            for (size_t p = 0; p < line.size();) {
                const size_t s2 = line.find(' ', p);
                f.push_back(line.substr(p, s2 == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : s2 - p));
                if (s2 == std::string::npos) break;
                p = s2 + 1;
            }
            if (f.size() < 3) { ink2_emit("ERR malformed GEN"); continue; }
            int max_new = std::atoi(f[1].c_str());
            std::vector<int> pr = parse_ids(f.back().c_str());
            Sampler s;
            bool cache_prompt = true;
            s.rng = std::move(smp.rng);
            std::vector<std::string> imgs;   // image=<path> keys, one per placeholder (ink2_vision.cuh)
            for (size_t k = 2; k + 1 < f.size(); ++k) {   // unknown keys are ignored
                const size_t eq = f[k].find('=');
                if (eq == std::string::npos) continue;
                const std::string key = f[k].substr(0, eq), val = f[k].substr(eq + 1);
                if (key == "temperature") s.temp = std::strtof(val.c_str(), nullptr);
                else if (key == "top_p") s.top_p = std::strtof(val.c_str(), nullptr);
                else if (key == "top_k") s.top_k = std::atoi(val.c_str());
                else if (key == "min_p") s.min_p = std::strtof(val.c_str(), nullptr);
                else if (key == "ignore_eos") s.ignore_eos = std::atoi(val.c_str()) != 0;
                else if (key == "cache_prompt") cache_prompt = std::atoi(val.c_str()) != 0;
                else if (key == "seed") s.rng.seed(std::strtoull(val.c_str(), nullptr, 10));
                else if (key == "image") imgs.push_back(ink2img::pct_decode(val));
            }
            if (pr.empty()) { ink2_emit("ERR empty prompt"); smp.rng = std::move(s.rng); continue; }
            if (const std::string ve = vis_prepare(pr, imgs); !ve.empty()) {   // placeholders -> image tokens
                ink2_emit("ERR image: %s", ve.c_str());
                smp.rng = std::move(s.rng);
                continue;
            }
            if ((int) pr.size() >= E.ctx) {
                ink2_emit("ERR prompt of %zu tokens exceeds the context %d", pr.size(), E.ctx);
                smp.rng = std::move(s.rng);
                continue;
            }
            max_new = std::max(0, std::min(max_new, E.ctx - (int) pr.size()));
            if (!cache_prompt) {
                hist.clear();
                last_logits.clear();
                if (g_sess.on) sess_discard_live();
                for (int si = 0; si < NCALLS; ++si) {
                    Slot& S = E.S[si];
                    CK(cudaMemsetAsync(S.khist, 0, (size_t) HR * KVD * sizeof(float), cudaStreamPerThread));
                    CK(cudaMemsetAsync(S.vhist, 0, (size_t) HR * KVD * sizeof(float), cudaStreamPerThread));
                    CK(cudaMemsetAsync(S.ahist, 0, (size_t) HR * H * sizeof(float), cudaStreamPerThread));
                    CK(cudaMemsetAsync(S.mhist, 0, (size_t) HR * H * sizeof(float), cudaStreamPerThread));
                }
                CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
            }
            const bool reuse = cache_prompt && !hist.empty() && pr.size() >= hist.size() && std::equal(hist.begin(), hist.end(), pr.begin());
            size_t start = reuse ? hist.size() : 0;
            if (g_sess.on && cache_prompt) start = sess_begin(pr, hist, last_logits, start);   // conversation cache (ink2_session.cuh)
            kv_trim((int) pr.size() + 1);
            if (spec && start == pr.size()) start = pr.size() - 1;   // re-fed: the drafter needs its features
            if (E.decode_misses) CK(cudaMemset(E.decode_misses, 0, NL * sizeof(unsigned long long)));
            if (PF.verify_cpu_unique) {
                CK(cudaMemsetAsync(PF.verify_cpu_unique, 0, NL * sizeof(unsigned), cudaStreamPerThread));
                CK(cudaMemsetAsync(PF.verify_cpu_rows, 0, NL * sizeof(unsigned), cudaStreamPerThread));
                CK(cudaStreamSynchronize(cudaStreamPerThread));
            }
            const double t0 = now_ms();
            const long acc0 = D.accepted, ver0 = D.verifies, off0 = D.offered;
            if (const std::string ve = vis_encode(start); !ve.empty()) {
                ink2_emit("ERR image: %s", ve.c_str());
                smp.rng = std::move(s.rng);
                continue;
            }
            if (start == pr.size()) {
                logits = last_logits;
            } else {
                slide_expand((int) start - 1);
                for (size_t p0 = start, Tz = 0; p0 < pr.size(); p0 += Tz) {
                    const int T = sess_chunk((int) p0, pf_fit((int) std::min((size_t) PF.TP, pr.size() - p0), (int) p0));
                    if (g_pf_full) { context_full(hist, p0, pr.size()); break; }   // ink2_grow_guard.cuh
                    Tz = (size_t) T;
                    if (T < g_pf_mapped_T) pf_unmap(false);
                    pf_map(T);
                    forward_prefill(&pr[p0], T, (int) p0, nullptr);
                    if (spec) {
                        draft_context(PF.feat, (int) p0, T);
                        if (p0 + T == pr.size()) draft_frnn(PF.feat + (size_t) (T - 1) * 4 * H);
                    }
                    if (cache_prompt) sess_after_chunk((int) (p0 + T));
                    const double ms = now_ms() - t0;
                    ink2_emit("PP %zu %zu %.0f %.1f", p0 + T, pr.size(), ms, 1000.0 * (p0 + T - start) / std::max(ms, 1.0));
                }
                if (g_ctx_full) {
                    g_ctx_full = false;
                    g_vis.pending = false;
                    smp.rng = std::move(s.rng);
                    continue;
                }
                CK(cudaMemcpy(logits.data(), E.logits, (size_t) VOCAB * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
                pf_unmap();
            if (spec && max_new > 0) slide_expand((int) pr.size() - 1);
            if (spec && max_new > 0 && g_spec_draft_n_max > 0 && g_pf_elastic) pf_map(g_spec_draft_n_max + 1);
            }
            if (cache_prompt) sess_prompt_end(pr, logits);
            const double t1 = now_ms();
            const double draft_ms0 = D.draft_ms, verify_ms0 = D.verify_ms;
            const size_t verify_staging0 = PF.fbytes;
            std::vector<int> gen;
            const char* finish = "length";
            int pos = (int) pr.size();
            if (spec && max_new > 0 && !stop.load()) {
                const int first = sample(logits, s);
                gen.push_back(first);
                ink2_emit("T %d", first);
                if (first == EOS_ID) finish = "stop";
                else spec_continue(first, pos, max_new, s, [&](int t) {
                    gen.push_back(t);
                    ink2_emit("T %d", t);
                    if (t == EOS_ID) { finish = "stop"; return false; }
                    return true;
                }, &stop);
                if (stop.load() && std::strcmp(finish, "stop") != 0) finish = "cancel";
            }
            for (int k = 0; k < (spec ? 0 : max_new); ++k) {
                if (stop.load()) { finish = "cancel"; break; }
                const int next = sample(logits, s);
                gen.push_back(next);
                ink2_emit("T %d", next);
                if (next == EOS_ID) { finish = "stop"; break; }
                if (k + 1 < max_new) {
                    if (!kv_room(pos + 1)) { std::fprintf(stderr, "kv-grow: the answer ends at %d tokens: the K/V cannot grow\n", pos); break; }
                    step(next, pos++, true);
                }
            }
            if (spec_diag && spec) {
                const long verifies = D.verifies - ver0;
                const long accepted = D.accepted - acc0;
                const long offered = D.offered - off0;
                unsigned cpu_unique[NL] = {}, cpu_rows[NL] = {};
                if (PF.verify_cpu_unique) {
                    CK(cudaMemcpy(cpu_unique, PF.verify_cpu_unique, sizeof(cpu_unique), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
                    CK(cudaMemcpy(cpu_rows, PF.verify_cpu_rows, sizeof(cpu_rows), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
                }
                unsigned core_unique = 0, outer_unique = 0, core_rows = 0, outer_rows = 0;
                for (int l = 0; l < NL; ++l) {
                    const bool core = l >= LOOP_BEGIN && l < LOOP_END;
                    (core ? core_unique : outer_unique) += cpu_unique[l];
                    (core ? core_rows : outer_rows) += cpu_rows[l];
                }
                const double mean_verifies = std::max(1L, verifies);
                const size_t staged = PF.fbytes - verify_staging0;
                std::fprintf(stderr,
                             "INK2_SPEC_METRICS n_max=%d rows=%d graph=%d verifies=%ld accepted=%ld offered=%ld acceptance=%.4f tokens_per_verify=%.3f draft_ms=%.2f verify_ms=%.2f staged_mb_per_verify=%.4f cpu_unique_core_per_verify=%.3f cpu_unique_outer_per_verify=%.3f cpu_routes_core_per_verify=%.3f cpu_routes_outer_per_verify=%.3f\n",
                             g_spec_draft_n_max, g_spec_draft_n_max + 1, E.graph, verifies, accepted, offered,
                             offered ? (double) accepted / offered : 0.0,
                             verifies ? 1.0 + (double) accepted / verifies : 0.0,
                             (D.draft_ms - draft_ms0) / mean_verifies, (D.verify_ms - verify_ms0) / mean_verifies,
                             staged / 1e6 / mean_verifies, core_unique / mean_verifies, outer_unique / mean_verifies,
                             core_rows / mean_verifies, outer_rows / mean_verifies);
            }
            const double t2 = now_ms();
            if (spec && g_spec_draft_n_max > 0 && g_pf_elastic) pf_unmap();
            if (E.decode_misses) {
                unsigned long long misses[NL] = {};
                CK(cudaMemcpy(misses, E.decode_misses, sizeof(misses), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
                std::fprintf(stderr, "INK2_DECODE_MISSES");
                for (int l = 0; l < NL; ++l) std::fprintf(stderr, " %d:%llu", l, misses[l]);
                std::fputc('\n', stderr);
            }
            g_vis.pending = false;
            if (cache_prompt) {
                hist = pr;
                if (!gen.empty()) hist.insert(hist.end(), gen.begin(), gen.end() - 1);   // the last token was never fed
                last_logits = logits;
            } else {
                hist.clear();
                last_logits.clear();
            }
            smp.rng = std::move(s.rng);
            // + drafts accepted / offered, prompt tokens not read (DONE fields 6-8: usage.cached_tokens)
            ink2_emit("DONE %zu %zu %.0f %.0f %s %ld %ld %zu", gen.size(), pr.size(), t1 - t0, t2 - t1, finish,
                      D.accepted - acc0, D.offered - off0, start);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    if (!vis_paths.empty() || std::count(ids.begin(), ids.end(), IMAGE_TOKEN)) {   // pictures (ink2_vision.cuh)
        std::string ve = PF.TP > 0 ? vis_prepare(ids, vis_paths) : "pictures need --chunk > 0";
        if (ve.empty() && (int) ids.size() + n_gen > E.ctx) ve = "prompt + n exceeds --ctx " + std::to_string(E.ctx);
        if (ve.empty()) ve = vis_encode(0);
        if (!ve.empty()) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", ve.c_str()); return 1; }
    }
    if (spec) {   // greedy speculative decoding: prompt in chunks, then draft and verify
        const double s0 = now_ms();
        for (size_t p0 = 0; p0 < ids.size();) {
            int T = (int) std::min((size_t) PF.TP, ids.size() - p0);
            if (g_pf_elastic) {
                T = pf_fit(T, (int) p0);
                if (T < g_pf_mapped_T) pf_unmap(false);
                pf_map(T);
            }
            forward_prefill(&ids[p0], T, (int) p0, nullptr);
            draft_context(PF.feat, (int) p0, T);
            if (p0 + T == ids.size()) draft_frnn(PF.feat + (size_t) (T - 1) * 4 * H);
            p0 += T;
        }
        if (g_pf_elastic) {
            pf_unmap();
            slide_expand((int) ids.size() - 1);
            if (g_spec_draft_n_max > 0) pf_map(g_spec_draft_n_max + 1);
        }
        CK(cudaMemcpy(logits.data(), E.logits, (size_t) VOCAB * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        const double s1 = now_ms();
        const size_t prompt_staging = PF.fbytes;
        Sampler smp;   // INK2_TEMP / INK2_SEED (tests): sampled speculative decoding
        if (const char* t = std::getenv("INK2_TEMP")) smp.temp = std::strtof(t, nullptr);
        if (const char* t = std::getenv("INK2_SEED")) smp.rng.seed(std::strtoull(t, nullptr, 10));
        std::vector<int> out = {sample(logits, smp)};
        spec_continue(out[0], (int) ids.size(), n_gen, smp, [&](int t) { out.push_back(t); return true; }, nullptr);
        CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
        const double s2 = now_ms();
        out.resize(std::min((size_t) n_gen, out.size()));
        std::printf("gen:");
        for (int t : out) std::printf(" %d", t);
        std::printf("\nprompt %zu tokens %.0f ms (%.1f tok/s), decode %zu tokens %.0f ms (%.1f tok/s)\n", ids.size(),
                    s1 - s0, 1000.0 * ids.size() / (s1 - s0), out.size(), s2 - s1, 1000.0 * out.size() / (s2 - s1));
        std::printf("spec: %ld verifies, %.3f acceptance (%.2f drafts accepted per verify, %.2f offered); accepted-count histogram:",
                    D.verifies, (double) D.accepted / std::max(1L, D.offered),
                    (double) D.accepted / std::max(1L, D.verifies), (double) D.offered / std::max(1L, D.verifies));
        for (int k = 0; k < DBLK; ++k) std::printf(" %ld", D.hk[k]);
        std::printf("; %ld plain tokens, %ld switches to plain, %ld requests gated\n", D.plain, D.switches, D.gated);
        if (E.lazy_experts) {
            const size_t verify_staging = PF.fbytes - prompt_staging;
            std::printf("verify expert staging: %.1f MB/token (%.1f MB total)\n",
                        verify_staging / 1e6 / std::max<size_t>(1, out.size()), verify_staging / 1e6);
        }
        if (D.verifies)
            std::printf("mean spec phase per verify: draft %.1f ms, target verify %.1f ms, remaining %.1f ms\n",
                        D.draft_ms / D.verifies, D.verify_ms / D.verifies,
                        (D.spec_ms - D.draft_ms - D.verify_ms) / D.verifies);
        if (g_pf_elastic) pf_unmap();
        if (debug_step_logits) std::fclose(debug_step_logits);
        if (debug_verify_logits) std::fclose(debug_verify_logits);
        return 0;
    }
    FILE* fg = dump_logits.empty() ? nullptr : std::fopen(dump_logits.c_str(), "wb");
    const double t0 = now_ms();
    if (PF.TP > 0) {   // chunked prefill
        std::vector<float> all(fg ? (size_t) PF.TP * VOCAB : 0);
        for (size_t p0 = 0, T = 0; p0 < ids.size(); p0 += T) {
            T = (size_t) pf_fit((int) std::min((size_t) PF.TP, ids.size() - p0), (int) p0);
            if ((int) T < g_pf_mapped_T) pf_unmap(false);
            pf_map((int) T);
            forward_prefill(&ids[p0], T, (int) p0, fg ? all.data() : nullptr);
            if (fg) std::fwrite(all.data(), 4, (size_t) T * VOCAB, fg);
        }
        CK(cudaMemcpy(logits.data(), E.logits, (size_t) VOCAB * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        pf_unmap();
    } else {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < ids.size(); ++t) {
            step(ids[t], (int) t, fg != nullptr || t + 1 == ids.size());
            if (fg) std::fwrite(logits.data(), 4, logits.size(), fg);
        }
    }
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    const double t1 = now_ms();
    if (fg) std::fclose(fg);
    if (E.grow) kv_fit((int) ids.size() + n_gen);
    write_placement_file(export_placement_path);
    apply_placement_file(placement_path);
    if (!placement_path.empty()) warm_cpu_workers((int) ids.size(), n_gen);
    const double t_decode0 = now_ms();
    unsigned long long fb1 = 0, fb2 = 0;
    if (E.offload) CK(cudaMemcpy(&fb1, E.fbytes, 8, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    std::printf("gen:");
    int pos = (int) ids.size();
    if (!replay_tokens_path.empty()) {
        for (int next : replay_ids) {
            std::printf(" %d", next);
            step(next, pos++, false);
        }
    } else {
        for (int k = 0; k < n_gen; ++k) {
            const int next = (int) (std::max_element(logits.begin(), logits.end()) - logits.begin());
            std::printf(" %d", next);
            step(next, pos++, true);
        }
    }
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    const double t2 = now_ms();
    if (E.offload) {
        CK(cudaMemcpy(&fb2, E.fbytes, 8, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        size_t fr = 0, tot = 0;
        CK(cudaMemGetInfo(&fr, &tot));
        std::printf("\nexpert fetch: prompt %.1f MB/token, decode %.1f MB/token; VRAM used %.2f GiB of %.2f\n",
                    (fb1 + PF.fbytes) / 1e6 / std::max<size_t>(1, ids.size()), (fb2 - fb1) / 1e6 / std::max(1, n_gen),
                    (tot - fr) / 1073741824.0, tot / 1073741824.0);
    }
    if (E.rec_mode == 1) {
        std::vector<int> r(rec_n);
        CK(cudaMemcpy(r.data(), E.rec, rec_n * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        FILE* f = std::fopen(rec_path.c_str(), "wb");
        if (!f || std::fwrite(r.data(), sizeof(int), rec_n, f) != rec_n) {
            std::fprintf(stderr, "cannot write routing record %s\n", rec_path.c_str());
            return 1;
        }
        std::fclose(f);
    }
    if (E.wait_ticks) {
        std::vector<unsigned long long> ticks((size_t) NCALLS * E.ctx);
        CK(cudaMemcpy(ticks.data(), E.wait_ticks, ticks.size() * sizeof(unsigned long long), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        int clock_khz = 0;
        CK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&clock_khz, cudaDevAttrClockRate, 0));
        std::ofstream f(wait_trace_path);
        if (!f) { std::fprintf(stderr, "cannot write wait trace %s\n", wait_trace_path.c_str()); return 1; }
        f << "position,visit,layer,wait_ticks,wait_us,cpu_us_per_job\n";
        for (int slot = 0; slot < NCALLS; ++slot) {
            const int layer = slot < NL ? slot : LOOP_BEGIN + (slot - NL) % NLOOP;
            float cpu_us = 0.0f;
            for (int n = 0; n < E.cpu_nodes; ++n)
                if (E.bell[n]) cpu_us += E.bell[n]->us_job_layer[layer] / E.cpu_nodes;
            if (!(cpu_us > 0.0f)) {
                for (int n = 0; n < E.cpu_nodes; ++n)
                    if (E.bell[n]) cpu_us += (layer < MXFP8_FIRST ? E.bell[n]->us_job_nv : E.bell[n]->us_job_mx) / E.cpu_nodes;
            }
            for (int p = (int) ids.size(); p < (int) ids.size() + n_gen; ++p) {
                const unsigned long long value = ticks[(size_t) slot * E.ctx + p];
                const double us = value / (clock_khz / 1000.0);
                f << p << ',' << slot << ',' << layer << ',' << value << ',' << us << ',' << cpu_us << '\n';
            }
        }
    }
    if (debug_step_logits) std::fclose(debug_step_logits);
    if (debug_verify_logits) std::fclose(debug_verify_logits);
    std::printf("\nprompt %zu tokens %.0f ms (%.1f tok/s), decode %d tokens %.0f ms (%.1f tok/s)\n", ids.size(), t1 - t0,
                1000.0 * ids.size() / (t1 - t0), n_gen, t2 - t_decode0,
                n_gen > 0 ? 1000.0 * n_gen / (t2 - t_decode0) : 0.0);
    return 0;
}

namespace ink2 {
int engine_main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    return ink2_engine_main(argc, argv);
}
}  // namespace ink2

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_engine.cu =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_grow_guard.cuh =====
// --kv-grow with the expert offload: the K/V and the prompt buffers take their memory from the expert pools.  These checks answer beforehand whether a growth fits: pf_fit reads a prompt in smaller
// chunks while one does not, and the serve loop ends a request the card cannot hold with an error (a prompt) or "length" (an answer) instead of
// the process stopping.  Nothing here runs without --kv-grow.
// CBA: --spec with --kv-grow (resident only in setup) still grows through kv_fit unchecked; its verify reads the prompt path

bool g_pf_full = false;    // pf_fit: even a 256-token chunk would leave a pool under its minimum
bool g_ctx_full = false;   // context_full ended the request (the serve loop goes on to the next one)
void pf_unmap();

// would pools_release(bytes) leave every pool FETCH_MAX slots?  The same arithmetic, nothing released
bool pools_keep_min(double bytes) {
    if (bytes <= 0) return true;
    if (!E.offload) return false;
    return pool_shrink_plan(bytes).fits;
}

// can the K/V grow to `need` cells (kv_fit's step) without a pool falling under its minimum?
bool kv_room(int need) {
    if (!E.grow || need <= E.kv_cells) return true;
    const int nc = std::min(E.ctx, (need + E.grow_step - 1) / E.grow_step * E.grow_step);
    const size_t add = kv_mapped_for(nc) - kv_mapped_for(E.kv_cells), keep = vram_keep(), fr = vram_free();
    return fr >= add + keep || pools_keep_min((double) (add + keep - fr));
}

// the serve loop's end of a prompt the card cannot hold: the buffers and the K/V beyond the default step back to the pools, nothing of this
// prompt kept as conversation (the K/V holds part of it), an ERR line for the server (no DONE follows)
void context_full(std::vector<int>& hist, size_t read, size_t prompt) {
    pf_unmap();
    kv_trim(1);
    hist.clear();
    g_pf_full = false;
    g_ctx_full = true;
    std::fprintf(stderr, "kv-grow: the prompt does not fit, %zu of %zu tokens read; expert slots %d + %d\n", read, prompt, E.nslot_nv,
                 E.nslot_mx);
    ink2_emit("ERR the context is full: the K/V of this %zu-token prompt does not fit beside the expert pools on this card (%zu tokens read); "
              "a shorter conversation fits", prompt, read);
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_grow_guard.cuh =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_image.cpp =====
// src/inkling2/ink2_image.cpp - see ink2_image.hpp.
#include "ink2_image.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>

#define STB_IMAGE_IMPLEMENTATION
#define STBI_ONLY_JPEG
#define STBI_ONLY_PNG
#define STBI_ONLY_BMP
#define STBI_ONLY_GIF
#define STBI_NO_LINEAR
#define STBI_NO_HDR
#include "../../vendor/stb/stb_image.h"

namespace ink2img {
namespace {

int tokens_for(int w, int h) { return ((h + TILE - 1) / TILE) * ((w + TILE - 1) / TILE); }

// area average (each output pixel the mean of the source area it covers, partial pixels weighted)
// CBA: the engine's own downscale for pictures over the token cap (the reference processor never resizes, so there is
// nothing to match); a PIL-style antialiased bicubic would only matter if the cap is hit often
void area_resize(const std::vector<uint8_t>& src, int sw, int sh, std::vector<uint8_t>& dst, int dw, int dh) {
    dst.assign((size_t) dw * dh * 3, 0);
    const double fx = (double) sw / dw, fy = (double) sh / dh;
    for (int oy = 0; oy < dh; ++oy) {
        const double y0 = oy * fy, y1 = (oy + 1) * fy;
        for (int ox = 0; ox < dw; ++ox) {
            const double x0 = ox * fx, x1 = (ox + 1) * fx;
            double acc[3] = {0, 0, 0}, area = 0;
            for (int iy = (int) y0; iy < std::min(sh, (int) std::ceil(y1)); ++iy) {
                const double wy = std::min(y1, iy + 1.0) - std::max(y0, (double) iy);
                for (int ix = (int) x0; ix < std::min(sw, (int) std::ceil(x1)); ++ix) {
                    const double wgt = wy * (std::min(x1, ix + 1.0) - std::max(x0, (double) ix));
                    const uint8_t* s = &src[((size_t) iy * sw + ix) * 3];
                    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) acc[c] += wgt * s[c];
                    area += wgt;
                }
            }
            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c)
                dst[((size_t) oy * dw + ox) * 3 + c] = (uint8_t) std::min(255.0, std::max(0.0, std::round(acc[c] / area)));
        }
    }
}

// patches from a tile accessor px(tile, y, x, c) (y, x within the 40 x 40 tile)
template <class F>
void cut(Picture& p, F px) {
    const int nt = p.rows * p.cols;
    p.patches.assign((size_t) nt * 4 * PIN, 0.0f);
    for (int t = 0; t < nt; ++t)
        for (int ir = 0; ir < 2; ++ir)
            for (int ic = 0; ic < 2; ++ic) {
                float* o = &p.patches[((size_t) t * 4 + ir * 2 + ic) * PIN];
                for (int y = 0; y < PATCH; ++y)
                    for (int x = 0; x < PATCH; ++x)
                        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) *o++ = px(t, ir * PATCH + y, ic * PATCH + x, c);
            }
}

}  // namespace

std::string load_picture(const std::string& path, int max_tokens, Picture& p) {
    int w = 0, h = 0, comp = 0;
    if (!stbi_info(path.c_str(), &w, &h, &comp)) return "cannot decode " + path + ": " + stbi_failure_reason();
    if ((int64_t) w * h > ((int64_t) 1 << 27))   // 134 MP (16 bytes a pixel while it is read: ~2 GB of RAM)
        return "picture of " + std::to_string(w) + "x" + std::to_string(h) + " px: more than 134 million pixels";
    unsigned char* px = stbi_load(path.c_str(), &w, &h, &comp, 4);
    if (!px) return "cannot decode " + path + ": " + stbi_failure_reason();
    if (w < 1 || h < 1) { stbi_image_free(px); return "empty picture " + path; }
    // the processor's convert_to_rgb: alpha over a checkerboard of 8 px squares, 204 where (row/8 + col/8) is even, else
    // 153, integer rounding as (rgb * a + bg * (255 - a) + 127) / 255 (an opaque pixel stays as it is)
    std::vector<uint8_t> rgb((size_t) w * h * 3);
    for (int y = 0; y < h; ++y)
        for (int x = 0; x < w; ++x) {
            const unsigned char* s = px + ((size_t) y * w + x) * 4;
            const int a = s[3], bg = ((y / 8 + x / 8) % 2 == 0) ? 204 : 153;
            for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) rgb[((size_t) y * w + x) * 3 + c] = (uint8_t) ((s[c] * a + bg * (255 - a) + 127) / 255);
        }
    stbi_image_free(px);
    p.src_w = w;
    p.src_h = h;
    if (max_tokens > 0 && tokens_for(w, h) > max_tokens) {
        double s = std::sqrt((double) max_tokens * TILE * TILE / ((double) w * h));
        int nw, nh;
        for (;; s *= 0.995) {
            nw = std::max(1, (int) (w * s));
            nh = std::max(1, (int) (h * s));
            if (tokens_for(nw, nh) <= max_tokens) break;
        }
        std::vector<uint8_t> small;
        area_resize(rgb, w, h, small, nw, nh);
        rgb.swap(small);
        w = nw;
        h = nh;
    }
    p.w = w;
    p.h = h;
    p.rows = (h + TILE - 1) / TILE;
    p.cols = (w + TILE - 1) / TILE;
    uint64_t k = 1469598103934665603ull;
    auto mix = [&k](const void* d, size_t n) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) { k ^= ((const uint8_t*) d)[i]; k *= 1099511628211ull; }
    };
    mix(&w, sizeof w);
    mix(&h, sizeof h);
    mix(rgb.data(), rgb.size());
    p.key = k;
    const float pad = (-1.0f - 127.5f) / 127.5f;   // the processor pads with -1 on the 0-255 scale, then normalizes
    cut(p, [&](int t, int y, int x, int c) {
        const int gy = (t / p.cols) * TILE + y, gx = (t % p.cols) * TILE + x;
        if (gy >= h || gx >= w) return pad;
        return ((float) rgb[((size_t) gy * w + gx) * 3 + c] - 127.5f) / 127.5f;
    });
    return "";
}

std::string load_pixels(const std::string& path, Picture& p) {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "rb");
    if (!f) return "cannot open " + path;
    int32_t rc[2] = {0, 0};
    std::vector<float> t;
    std::string err;
    if (std::fread(rc, 4, 2, f) != 2 || rc[0] < 1 || rc[1] < 1) err = "bad pixel file " + path;
    else {
        t.resize((size_t) rc[0] * rc[1] * TILE * TILE * 3);
        if (std::fread(t.data(), 4, t.size(), f) != t.size()) err = "short pixel file " + path;
    }
    std::fclose(f);
    if (!err.empty()) return err;
    p.rows = rc[0];
    p.cols = rc[1];
    p.w = p.src_w = p.cols * TILE;
    p.h = p.src_h = p.rows * TILE;
    p.key = 0;
    cut(p, [&](int tile, int y, int x, int c) { return t[(((size_t) tile * TILE + y) * TILE + x) * 3 + c]; });
    return "";
}

std::string pct_decode(const std::string& s) {
    std::string o;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < s.size(); ++i) {
        if (s[i] == '%' && i + 2 < s.size()) {
            const std::string hx = s.substr(i + 1, 2);
            char* e = nullptr;
            const long v = std::strtol(hx.c_str(), &e, 16);
            if (e && *e == '\0') { o += (char) v; i += 2; continue; }
        }
        o += s[i];
    }
    return o;
}

}  // namespace ink2img

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_image.cpp =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_image.hpp =====
// src/inkling2/ink2_image.hpp - Inkling-2's pictures on the host (plain C++, no GPU): decode, the processor's
// preprocessing, and the ViT's input patches.  The ViT itself is ink2_vision.cuh.
//
// transformers' Inkling2ImageProcessor (huggingface/transformers#51050) never resizes: the picture is padded at the
// right and the bottom to whole 40 x 40 tiles with -1 on the 0-255 scale, scaled to [-1, 1] ((p - 127.5) / 127.5),
// and each tile is one image token (rows x cols tokens, raster order).  The ViT cuts a tile into 2 x 2 native patches
// of 20 x 20 pixels, each a 1200-vector in (y, x, channel) order.  A picture with an alpha channel is composited onto
// the processor's checkerboard (8 px squares of 204 / 153) first.
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace ink2img {

constexpr int TILE = 40, PATCH = 20, PIN = 3 * PATCH * PATCH;   // 1200 inputs per native patch

struct Picture {
    int w = 0, h = 0;             // the size the ViT reads (after the token cap)
    int src_w = 0, src_h = 0;     // as decoded
    int rows = 0, cols = 0;       // image tokens: ceil(h / 40) x ceil(w / 40)
    uint64_t key = 0;             // FNV-1a of the pixels the ViT reads and their size (what prefix reuse compares)
    std::vector<float> patches;   // [rows * cols * 4][1200]: per tile (raster order) its 2 x 2 patches, row-major
};

// Decodes a JPEG / PNG / BMP / GIF (first frame) file and prepares it.  A picture over `max_tokens` image tokens is
// scaled down to fit first (the reference never resizes: this is the engine's own cap).  "" or the error.
std::string load_picture(const std::string& path, int max_tokens, Picture& p);

// The processor's own pixel tensor (terminal/tools/inkling2/vision_oracle.py .pix: int32 rows, int32 cols, then
// float32 [rows * cols][40][40][3]), cut into the ViT's patches.  "" or the error.
std::string load_pixels(const std::string& path, Picture& p);

// %20 -> space, %25 -> % (the protocol's image=<path> keys, as serve/hy4_kind.py encode_path writes them)
std::string pct_decode(const std::string& s);

}  // namespace ink2img

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_image.hpp =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_main.cpp =====
#include "ink2_embedded.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <exception>

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    try {
        return ink2::engine_main(argc, argv);
    } catch (const std::exception & e) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "inkling2 engine: %s\n", e.what());
        return 1;
    }
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_main.cpp =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_session.cuh =====
// The conversation cache of --serve (--conversation-cache-mib N, off by default): agentic use (one agent over many
// turns, an agent and its sub-agents taking turns) does not read again a prompt it has already read.
//
// The slots hold one conversation (the live one, `hist` of the serve loop).  Its state after n tokens is, per layer
// visit: the full-attention K / V rows [0, n) (8 visits), the sliding-window rows of positions [n - WINDOW, n) in
// their ring (40 visits), the four causal-conv rings (khist / vhist / ahist / mhist, HR rows each, 48 visits); with
// --spec the drafter's K / V (its global layer: rows [0, n), its two local layers: their ring's window); and the
// logits after token n - 1.  Every --kv format is bytes per row here (codes and scales alike, kv_cell_bytes).  Not
// per conversation, not saved: the expert pools and the CPU experts' placement, the drafter's Markov context (the
// serve loop always reads at least the prompt's last token with --spec, which recomputes it), the sampler.
//
// A checkpoint (Ck) is the "tail" of a state at n: everything above except the full-attention rows, ~190 MiB at f16,
// in page-locked host memory.  The full rows [0, n) are free to keep: a later position never writes them.  So:
//   - at each prompt's end (before the first sampled token) a checkpoint of the live conversation: a client that
//     sends the history back re-rendered (its reasoning dropped, its tool call re-serialized) diverges from the
//     generated tokens after the prompt, and the next request restores that point instead of reading from 0;
//   - a request that does not extend the live conversation's last prompt parks it (its full rows, its checkpoints
//     and one at its history end) and restores the parked conversation, live checkpoint or branch point that covers
//     the longest prefix of the request;
//   - a request sharing a long prefix with a known conversation without a checkpoint there (sub-agents sharing a
//     system prompt and tools) stops its prefill at the branch point once and takes a checkpoint there.
// LRU over whole parked conversations under the budget.  Off: none of this runs (the serve loop is the base one).

constexpr int SPLIT_MIN = 512;     // a branch point is taken when it covers this many tokens more than the best state

struct Pinned {   // page-locked host memory (DMA only: any NUMA node)
    uint8_t* p = nullptr;
    size_t n = 0;
    explicit Pinned(size_t bytes) {
        if (bytes && cudaHostAlloc((void**) &p, bytes, cudaHostAllocDefault) == cudaSuccess) n = bytes;
        else { p = nullptr; (void) cudaGetLastError(); }
    }
    ~Pinned() { if (p) cudaFreeHost(p); }
    Pinned(const Pinned&) = delete;
    Pinned& operator=(const Pinned&) = delete;
};
using PinnedP = std::shared_ptr<Pinned>;

struct Ck {             // the tail of the state after the first n tokens
    int n = 0;
    PinnedP tail;
    vec logits;
    int kind = 0;          // CK_PROMPT a prompt's end, CK_BRANCH a branch point, CK_END a parked history's end
};
constexpr int CK_PROMPT = 0, CK_BRANCH = 1, CK_END = 2;
struct Conv {           // a parked conversation: its tokens, its full rows [0, ids.size()), its checkpoints
    std::vector<int> ids;
    PinnedP full;
    std::vector<Ck> cks;
    int prompt_n = 0;      // its last prompt's length: a request extending it continues this conversation
    uint64_t used = 0;
    bool hold = false;     // the one sess_begin restores: not evicted meanwhile
};

struct SessArr { uint8_t* d; size_t b; int cap; };   // device rows of b bytes, position q in row q % cap

struct Sess {
    bool on = false;
    size_t budget = 0;     // bytes
    int min_tokens = 512;  // --conversation-cache-min-tokens: shorter conversations are not parked
    std::vector<SessArr> full, ring;
    std::vector<std::pair<void*, size_t>> conv;
    size_t tail_bytes = 0, row_bytes = 0;
    std::vector<Ck> live;  // checkpoints of the live conversation (its full rows are the slots')
    int live_prompt = 0;   // the live conversation's last prompt length (0: none)
    std::vector<Conv> parked;
    std::vector<PinnedP> spare;   // tails no longer used (cudaHostAlloc is slow): taken again first
    uint64_t clock = 0;
    int split = -1;        // this request: the prefill stops here for a branch checkpoint
    std::string note;      // this request's log line
} g_sess;

void sess_discard_live() {
    g_sess.live.clear();
    g_sess.live_prompt = 0;
    g_sess.split = -1;
}

void sess_init(int mib, int min_tokens) {
    if (mib <= 0) return;
    g_sess.min_tokens = min_tokens;
    for (int s = 0; s < NCALLS; ++s) {
        Slot& S = E.S[s];
        void* arr[4] = {S.K, S.V, S.ksc, S.vsc};
        for (int a = 0; a < 4; ++a) {
            const size_t b = kv_cell_bytes(a);
            if (!b) continue;
            (S.full ? g_sess.full : g_sess.ring).push_back({(uint8_t*) arr[a], b, S.cap});
        }
        g_sess.conv.push_back({S.khist, (size_t) HR * KVD * 4});
        g_sess.conv.push_back({S.vhist, (size_t) HR * KVD * 4});
        g_sess.conv.push_back({S.ahist, (size_t) HR * H * 4});
        g_sess.conv.push_back({S.mhist, (size_t) HR * H * 4});
    }
    if (D.on)
        for (int i = 0; i < DL; ++i)
            for (__half* x : {D.L[i].K, D.L[i].V}) (D.L[i].local ? g_sess.ring : g_sess.full).push_back({(uint8_t*) x, (size_t) KVD * 2, D.L[i].cap});
    for (auto& a : g_sess.ring) g_sess.tail_bytes += (size_t) WINDOW * a.b;
    for (auto& c : g_sess.conv) g_sess.tail_bytes += c.second;
    for (auto& a : g_sess.full) g_sess.row_bytes += a.b;
    g_sess.budget = (size_t) mib << 20;
    if (g_sess.budget < 3 * g_sess.tail_bytes) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "--conversation-cache-mib %d: below %zu MiB (three checkpoints of %zu MiB): cache off\n", mib,
                     (3 * g_sess.tail_bytes) >> 20, g_sess.tail_bytes >> 20);
        return;
    }
    g_sess.on = true;
    std::fprintf(stderr, "conversation cache: %d MiB of host memory, checkpoints of %.0f MiB, %.1f KiB per parked token, "
                         "conversations of %d+ tokens parked\n", mib, g_sess.tail_bytes / 1048576.0, g_sess.row_bytes / 1024.0, min_tokens);
}

void sess_rebind() {
    if (!g_sess.on) return;
    g_sess.full.clear();
    g_sess.ring.clear();
    for (int s = 0; s < NCALLS; ++s) {
        Slot& S = E.S[s];
        void* arr[4] = {S.K, S.V, S.ksc, S.vsc};
        for (int a = 0; a < 4; ++a) {
            const size_t b = kv_cell_bytes(a);
            if (!b) continue;
            (S.full ? g_sess.full : g_sess.ring).push_back({(uint8_t*) arr[a], b, S.cap});
        }
    }
    if (D.on)
        for (int i = 0; i < DL; ++i)
            for (__half* x : {D.L[i].K, D.L[i].V}) (D.L[i].local ? g_sess.ring : g_sess.full).push_back({(uint8_t*) x, (size_t) KVD * 2, D.L[i].cap});
    g_sess.tail_bytes = 0;
    g_sess.row_bytes = 0;
    for (auto& a : g_sess.ring) g_sess.tail_bytes += (size_t) WINDOW * a.b;
    for (auto& c : g_sess.conv) g_sess.tail_bytes += c.second;
    for (auto& a : g_sess.full) g_sess.row_bytes += a.b;
}

size_t sess_lcp(const std::vector<int>& a, const std::vector<int>& b) {
    size_t l = 0;
    while (l < a.size() && l < b.size() && a[l] == b[l]) ++l;
    return l;
}
// positions [lo, hi) of an array <-> host bytes h (position order)
void sess_rows(const SessArr& a, int lo, int hi, uint8_t* h, bool d2h) {
    for (int q = lo; q < hi;) {
        const int r = q % a.cap, m = std::min(hi - q, a.cap - r);
        uint8_t* dv = a.d + (size_t) r * a.b;
        uint8_t* hv = h + (size_t) (q - lo) * a.b;
        CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d2h ? (void*) hv : (void*) dv, d2h ? (const void*) dv : (const void*) hv, (size_t) m * a.b,
                           d2h ? cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost : cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, cudaStreamPerThread));
        q += m;
    }
}
void sess_tail(int n, uint8_t* h, bool d2h) {   // the ring windows and conv rings of the state at n
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    const int lo = std::max(0, n - WINDOW);
    for (auto& a : g_sess.ring) { sess_rows(a, lo, n, h, d2h); h += (size_t) WINDOW * a.b; }
    for (auto& c : g_sess.conv) {
        CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d2h ? (void*) h : c.first, d2h ? (const void*) c.first : (const void*) h, c.second,
                           d2h ? cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost : cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, cudaStreamPerThread));
        h += c.second;
    }
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
}
void sess_full(int n, uint8_t* h, bool d2h) {   // the full rows [0, n), array after array
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    for (auto& a : g_sess.full) { sess_rows(a, 0, n, h, d2h); h += (size_t) n * a.b; }
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
}

// host bytes held: each tail once (branch checkpoints are shared between conversations)
size_t sess_used() {
    std::vector<const Pinned*> seen;
    size_t t = 0;
    auto tail = [&](const PinnedP& p) {
        if (p && std::find(seen.begin(), seen.end(), p.get()) == seen.end()) { seen.push_back(p.get()); t += p->n; }
    };
    for (auto& c : g_sess.live) tail(c.tail);
    for (auto& v : g_sess.parked) { t += v.full ? v.full->n : 0; for (auto& c : v.cks) tail(c.tail); }
    for (auto& p : g_sess.spare) tail(p);
    return t;
}
// room for `bytes` more: spare buffers freed first, then the least recently used parked conversations (not one held)
bool sess_room(size_t bytes) {
    while (sess_used() + bytes > g_sess.budget) {
        if (!g_sess.spare.empty()) { g_sess.spare.pop_back(); continue; }
        auto lru = g_sess.parked.end();
        for (auto it = g_sess.parked.begin(); it != g_sess.parked.end(); ++it)
            if (!it->hold && (lru == g_sess.parked.end() || it->used < lru->used)) lru = it;
        if (lru == g_sess.parked.end()) return false;
        g_sess.note += " evicted " + std::to_string(lru->ids.size());
        g_sess.parked.erase(lru);
    }
    return true;
}
// CBA: page-locked buffers are kept for reuse (cudaHostAlloc pins ~0.45 ms per MiB on a PCIe 3 host: 80 ms per
// checkpoint), a spare taken for anything up to half its size; checkpoints in PIN_UNIT steps, a parked conversation's
// rows in PIN_UNIT << k (a conversation that grew a turn finds its last buffer again).  Up to half a row buffer is
// slack; a sub-allocator in one pinned arena would waste less
constexpr size_t PIN_UNIT = (size_t) 32 << 20;
PinnedP sess_alloc(size_t bytes, bool geo = false) {
    bytes = (bytes + PIN_UNIT - 1) / PIN_UNIT * PIN_UNIT;
    if (geo) { size_t g = PIN_UNIT; while (g < bytes) g *= 2; bytes = g; }
    auto& sp = g_sess.spare;
    size_t bi = sp.size();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sp.size(); ++i)
        if (sp[i]->n >= bytes && sp[i]->n <= 2 * bytes && (bi == sp.size() || sp[i]->n < sp[bi]->n)) bi = i;
    if (bi < sp.size()) { PinnedP p = sp[bi]; sp.erase(sp.begin() + bi); return p; }
    if (!sess_room(bytes)) return nullptr;
    PinnedP p = std::make_shared<Pinned>(bytes);
    return p->p ? p : nullptr;
}
void sess_drop(PinnedP&& p) {   // a buffer nothing uses any more goes to the spares
    if (p && p.use_count() == 1) g_sess.spare.push_back(std::move(p));
    p.reset();
}
// a checkpoint of the live state at n (its logits: lg)
bool sess_take(int n, const vec& lg, int kind) {
    PinnedP t = sess_alloc(g_sess.tail_bytes);
    if (!t) { g_sess.note += " no-room"; return false; }
    sess_tail(n, t->p, true);
    g_sess.live.push_back({n, t, lg, kind});
    return true;
}

// The live conversation (hist, last_logits) to the parked set, when worth it: its full rows, its checkpoints and its
// history end.  The live checkpoints stay valid for the slots (a later restore may use them).
void sess_park(const std::vector<int>& hist, const vec& last_logits) {
    if ((int) hist.size() < g_sess.min_tokens) return;
    Conv v;
    v.ids = hist;
    v.prompt_n = g_sess.live_prompt;
    v.cks = g_sess.live;
    const bool end = std::none_of(v.cks.begin(), v.cks.end(), [&](const Ck& c) { return c.n == (int) hist.size(); });
    const size_t need = hist.size() * g_sess.row_bytes + (end ? g_sess.tail_bytes : 0);
    if (!sess_room(need)) { g_sess.note += " park-no-room"; return; }
    // in the parked set (held) while its buffers are taken, so that the budget counts them
    v.used = ++g_sess.clock;
    v.hold = true;
    const uint64_t id = v.used;
    g_sess.parked.push_back(std::move(v));
    auto me = [&]() -> Conv& { for (auto& c : g_sess.parked) if (c.used == id) return c; return g_sess.parked.back(); };
    PinnedP full = sess_alloc(hist.size() * g_sess.row_bytes, true);
    if (!full) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < g_sess.parked.size(); ++j) if (g_sess.parked[j].used == id) g_sess.parked.erase(g_sess.parked.begin() + j);
        g_sess.note += " park-no-room";
        return;
    }
    me().full = full;
    sess_full((int) hist.size(), full->p, true);
    if (end) {
        PinnedP t = sess_alloc(g_sess.tail_bytes);
        if (t) { sess_tail((int) hist.size(), t->p, true); me().cks.push_back({(int) hist.size(), t, last_logits, CK_END}); }
    }
    me().hold = false;
    g_sess.note += " parked " + std::to_string(hist.size());
}

// Before the request's kv_trim (a parked conversation's rows are still mapped): picks the longest prefix of pr with a
// state, parks the live conversation if pr does not continue it, restores that state.  `start` = the base loop's
// choice (hist.size() when pr extends hist, else 0); returns the position to read from.  hist / last_logits follow.
size_t sess_begin(const std::vector<int>& pr, std::vector<int>& hist, vec& last_logits, size_t start) {
    g_sess.split = -1;
    g_sess.note.clear();
    const double t0 = now_ms();
    // (a picture's image tokens are ids made from its pixels' hash, ink2_vision.cuh vis_id: every comparison below
    // tells two pictures of the same size apart)
    const size_t l_live = sess_lcp(hist, pr);
    // the best state: the live history end (start), a live checkpoint, a parked conversation's checkpoint
    size_t best = start, share = l_live;
    int src = start > 0 ? 0 : -1, ck = -1, pv = -1;   // src: 0 live end, 1 live checkpoint, 2 parked, -1 none
    for (size_t i = 0; i < g_sess.live.size(); ++i)
        if (g_sess.live[i].n <= (int) l_live && g_sess.live[i].n > (int) best) { best = g_sess.live[i].n; src = 1; ck = (int) i; }
    for (size_t j = 0; j < g_sess.parked.size(); ++j) {
        const size_t l = sess_lcp(g_sess.parked[j].ids, pr);
        share = std::max(share, l);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < g_sess.parked[j].cks.size(); ++i)
            if (g_sess.parked[j].cks[i].n <= (int) l && g_sess.parked[j].cks[i].n > (int) best) { best = g_sess.parked[j].cks[i].n; src = 2; ck = (int) i; pv = (int) j; }
    }
    // the live conversation is parked unless the request extends its last prompt (the same conversation, re-rendered
    // or continued)
    const bool same = (!hist.empty() && l_live == hist.size()) || (g_sess.live_prompt > 0 && (int) l_live >= g_sess.live_prompt);
    // a branch point between conversations only (a re-render diverging deep in the generated tail is not one)
    if (!same && share >= best + SPLIT_MIN && share < pr.size()) g_sess.split = (int) share;
    if (pv >= 0) g_sess.parked[pv].hold = true;
    if (!same && !hist.empty()) sess_park(hist, last_logits);   // (may evict parked conversations, not the held one)
    for (size_t j = 0; j < g_sess.parked.size(); ++j) if (g_sess.parked[j].hold) { pv = (int) j; g_sess.parked[j].hold = false; }
    const char* how = "live";
    if (src == 1) {   // the slots' own rows [0, n): the tail only
        const Ck c = g_sess.live[ck];
        sess_tail(c.n, c.tail->p, false);
        last_logits = c.logits;
        how = "rewind";
    } else if (src == 2) {
        Conv& v = g_sess.parked[pv];
        const Ck c = v.cks[ck];
        kv_fit(c.n + 1);   // --kv-grow: a conversation longer than the K/V mapped now
        if ((int) sess_lcp(hist, v.ids) < c.n || same) {   // (same: the slots' rows past the live prompt may differ)
            // the parked rows array after array: n rows of each, from an image of ids.size() rows each
            CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
            const uint8_t* h = v.full->p;
            for (auto& a : g_sess.full) { sess_rows(a, 0, c.n, const_cast<uint8_t*>(h), false); h += v.ids.size() * a.b; }
            CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
        }
        sess_tail(c.n, c.tail->p, false);
        last_logits = c.logits;
        how = "parked";
        v.used = ++g_sess.clock;
        // the request continues that conversation (extends its last prompt): it leaves the parked set, its checkpoints
        // up to n become the live ones; else (a sibling from a shared branch point) it stays parked, its branch
        // checkpoints shared
        std::vector<Ck> keep;
        for (auto& x : v.cks) if (x.n <= c.n && (x.n == c.n || x.kind == CK_BRANCH)) keep.push_back(x);
        g_sess.live = keep;
        if (v.prompt_n > 0 && (int) best >= v.prompt_n) {   // its buffers to the spares (unless shared with the live)
            Conv dead = std::move(v);
            g_sess.parked.erase(g_sess.parked.begin() + pv);
            sess_drop(std::move(dead.full));
            for (auto& x : dead.cks) sess_drop(std::move(x.tail));
        }
    } else if (src < 0) {
        how = "none";
    }
    for (size_t i = g_sess.live.size(); i-- > 0;)   // checkpoints past the read start are no longer the slots' state
        if (g_sess.live[i].n > (int) best) { PinnedP t = g_sess.live[i].tail; g_sess.live.erase(g_sess.live.begin() + i); sess_drop(std::move(t)); }
    g_sess.live_prompt = 0;   // (sess_prompt_end sets it)
    hist.assign(pr.begin(), pr.begin() + best);
    std::fprintf(stderr, "[ink2] conversation cache: prompt %zu, read from %zu (%s)%s%s, %zu parked, %.0f of %zu MiB, %.0f ms\n",
                 pr.size(), best, how, g_sess.split > 0 ? (", branch point " + std::to_string(g_sess.split)).c_str() : "",
                 g_sess.note.c_str(), g_sess.parked.size(), sess_used() / 1048576.0, g_sess.budget >> 20, now_ms() - t0);
    return best;
}

// the prefill chunk at p0 of T tokens: ends at the branch point when it crosses it
int sess_chunk(int p0, int T) { return g_sess.on && g_sess.split > p0 && g_sess.split < p0 + T ? g_sess.split - p0 : T; }
// after the chunk ending at p: the branch checkpoint.  CBA: the live conversation's checkpoints are never evicted
// (only parked conversations are); one branch point per request at most, they leave with it when it is parked
void sess_after_chunk(int p) {
    if (!g_sess.on || p != g_sess.split) return;
    vec lg(VOCAB);
    CK(cudaMemcpy(lg.data(), E.logits, (size_t) VOCAB * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    const double t0 = now_ms();
    if (sess_take(p, lg, CK_BRANCH)) std::fprintf(stderr, "[ink2] conversation cache: branch checkpoint at %d, %.0f ms\n", p, now_ms() - t0);
}
// FNV-1a of the logits: identity checks (serve mode has no logits dump)
unsigned long long sess_hash(const vec& lg) {
    uint64_t h = 1469598103934665603ull;
    for (float f : lg) { uint32_t u; std::memcpy(&u, &f, 4); h = (h ^ u) * 1099511628211ull; }
    return (unsigned long long) h;
}
// the prompt read (lg: its last logits), before the first sampled token: the live prompt-end checkpoint
void sess_prompt_end(const std::vector<int>& pr, const vec& lg) {
    if (!g_sess.on) {   // INK2_LOGITS_HASH=1: the hash without the cache too (the reference runs)
        static const bool h = std::getenv("INK2_LOGITS_HASH") != nullptr;
        if (h) std::fprintf(stderr, "[ink2] conversation cache: off, prompt end %zu, logits %016llx\n", pr.size(), sess_hash(lg));
        return;
    }
    const double t0 = now_ms();
    bool have = false;   // a checkpoint at this very point already (the same prompt again, a restored one)
    for (size_t i = g_sess.live.size(); i-- > 0;) {   // earlier prompt ends / a restored end (prefixes of this) give way
        if (g_sess.live[i].n == (int) pr.size()) { have = true; continue; }
        if (g_sess.live[i].kind != CK_BRANCH) { PinnedP t = g_sess.live[i].tail; g_sess.live.erase(g_sess.live.begin() + i); sess_drop(std::move(t)); }
    }
    g_sess.note.clear();
    g_sess.live_prompt = have || sess_take((int) pr.size(), lg, CK_PROMPT) ? (int) pr.size() : 0;
    std::fprintf(stderr, "[ink2] conversation cache: prompt end %zu checkpoint %.0f ms%s, %.0f MiB, logits %016llx\n", pr.size(),
                 now_ms() - t0, g_sess.note.c_str(), sess_used() / 1048576.0, sess_hash(lg));
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_session.cuh =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_vision.cuh =====
// src/inkling2/ink2_vision.cuh - Inkling-2's image input in bel-inkling2: the ViT + projector, and the image rows of a
// prompt.  Included by ink2_engine.cu inside its anonymous namespace, after the prompt path (Prefill, the expert pools).
//
// Model (checkpoint model.visual.*, transformers#51050 Inkling2VisionModel; checked in fp32 against it, see
// terminal/tools/inkling2/vision_oracle.py):
//   * input: the picture's 2 x 2 native patches of 20 x 20 px per image token (ink2_image.hpp), a 1200-vector each;
//     patch_embedding 1200 -> 1024 (no bias) + a learned 32 x 32 position table resampled bilinearly (align_corners
//     False, edge-clamped) to the native patch grid; a class token (class + its position embedding) in front;
//     LayerNorm (ln_pre).  temporal_patch_size is unused for a still picture.
//   * 23 pre-LayerNorm blocks, hidden 1024, 16 heads of 64, biased q/k/v/out, full attention within the picture,
//     2D axial RoPE (theta 1e4, 16 frequencies): channel pair j < 16 turns by (column + 1) * f_j, pair j >= 16 by
//     (row + 1) * f_{j-16}, interleaved pairs; the class token sits at (0, 0).  Layer scale on both branches
//     (x + ls_1 * attn, x + ls_2 * mlp), MLP 1024 -> 4096 -> 1024 with the exact (erf) GELU.  No final norm.
//   * projector: the 4 patch states of a token concatenated (4096) -> proj_in 4096 -> GELU -> proj_out 2304, no bias.
//   * the language model reads these rows in place of the image placeholders' rows AFTER its embedding norm (the
//     reference masked_scatter's them into inputs_embeds = embed_norm(embed(ids))); positions stay plain token indices.
//
// Numerics: fp32 throughout (cuBLAS SGEMM, pedantic math, on fp32 copies of the BF16 weights made per use).
// CBA: attention as SGEMM over query chunks with the scores in VRAM, fine to a few thousand ViT tokens (a 1000-token
// picture: ~0.2 s) but 7 s at the cap (16k ViT tokens, 4096 image tokens); a flash-style TF32 / fp16 kernel if big
// pictures matter.  One picture at a time, no batching across a prompt's pictures.
//
// Memory: nothing at load.  At the first picture the weights are read into pinned host memory (634 MB BF16 + 6 MB
// fp32) and a cuBLAS handle is made (a few MiB of VRAM: 7.90 GiB used at 8 GiB text-only, 7.91 GiB after a picture).
// The weights are streamed to the GPU matrix by matrix during each encode.  An encode's working VRAM (weight staging
// 100 MB + 20 KB per ViT token + 32 MB) comes from the tail of the NVFP4 expert pool when the experts are offloaded
// (those slots are evicted, then handed back empty: the pool refills them on demand), else from free VRAM beyond the
// margin, and goes back as the encode ends.
// CBA: the weights cross PCIe at every encode (~650 MB, about 90 ms on this box: most of a small picture's cost); a
// resident copy when VRAM is free (no offload) is the upgrade.
//
// Protocol (Hy4-Flash's 1.6b): GEN ... image=<path> ... <ids>, one image= key per < |unused_200054|> of the ids in
// prompt order; the engine decodes the files, expands each placeholder to its rows x cols image tokens and encodes
// them before the prompt is read.  INFO images=1 at the start says it can.
//
// Picture identity: an expanded image token is not 200054 in the prompt the engine keeps but a negative id made from
// the picture's key (its pixels' hash) and its offset in the picture (vis_id).  Every prefix comparison of the serve
// loop and of the conversation cache (hist, live checkpoints, parked conversations, branch points) therefore sees two
// different pictures of the same size as different tokens: a state is reused only with the same pictures at the same
// places.  Only the embedding lookup (forward_prefill) maps a negative id back to the placeholder's row, which the
// picture's row then replaces.
#pragma once

constexpr int VH = 1024, VHEADS = 16, VHD = 64, VFF = 4096, VLAYERS = 23, VGRID = 32, VMERGE_IN = 4 * VH, VPROJ = 4096;
constexpr float VEPS = 1e-5f;
constexpr int IMAGE_TOKEN = 200054;   // < |unused_200054|> (processor_config.json image_token)
constexpr size_t VSC = (size_t) 8 << 20;   // scores / MLP scratch, floats (32 MB): attention in query chunks of VSC / S
// small fp32 tensors of one layer, offsets
constexpr int SM_BQ = 0, SM_BK = 1024, SM_BV = 2048, SM_BO = 3072, SM_B1 = 4096, SM_B2 = 8192, SM_LN1W = 9216,
              SM_LN1B = 10240, SM_LN2W = 11264, SM_LN2B = 12288, SM_LS1 = 13312, SM_LS2 = 14336, SM_N = 15360;

struct Vis {
    int max_tokens = 4096;     // --image-max-tokens: per picture (bigger ones are scaled down first)
    std::string dump;          // --dump-image-emb: the image rows of the last encode, float32 [n][2304]
    // weights (pinned host, loaded at the first picture)
    bool loaded = false;
    uint16_t* wb = nullptr;    // the BF16 matrices back to back
    float* ws = nullptr;       // the small fp32 tensors
    size_t nb = 0, ns = 0;
    size_t m_patch = 0, m_in = 0, m_out = 0, m_l[VLAYERS][6] = {};   // matrices (q k v o fc1 fc2) in wb
    size_t s_pos = 0, s_cls = 0, s_lnw = 0, s_lnb = 0, s_l[VLAYERS] = {};
    cublasHandle_t cb = nullptr;
    // the request
    struct Span { int pos, n; uint64_t key; size_t row; };
    std::vector<ink2img::Picture> pics;
    std::vector<Span> spans;         // this prompt's pictures
    float* emb = nullptr;            // pinned [rows][H]: the image rows of this prompt
    size_t emb_cap = 0;
    bool pending = false;            // the prompt being read has image rows to put in
} g_vis;

double vis_ms() {
    return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch()).count();
}

bool vis_available() { return !g_is_gguf && E.ck && E.ck->st.tensors.count("model.visual.proj_out.weight"); }

void vis_load() {
    if (g_vis.loaded) return;
    const double t0 = vis_ms();
    const std::string P = "model.visual.", B = P + "backbone.";
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, size_t*>> mats = {{B + "embeddings.patch_embedding.weight", &g_vis.m_patch},
                                                         {P + "proj_in.weight", &g_vis.m_in}, {P + "proj_out.weight", &g_vis.m_out}};
    const char* lm[6] = {"self_attn.q_proj", "self_attn.k_proj", "self_attn.v_proj", "self_attn.out_proj", "mlp.c_fc", "mlp.c_proj"};
    for (int l = 0; l < VLAYERS; ++l)
        for (int j = 0; j < 6; ++j) mats.push_back({B + "layers." + std::to_string(l) + "." + lm[j] + ".weight", &g_vis.m_l[l][j]});
    size_t nb = 0;
    for (auto& m : mats) {
        const SafeTensor& t = E.ck->t(m.first);
        if (t.dtype != "BF16") { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s: %s, expected BF16\n", m.first.c_str(), t.dtype.c_str()); std::exit(1); }
        *m.second = nb;
        nb += (t.end - t.begin) / 2;
    }
    g_vis.ns = (size_t) VGRID * VGRID * VH + 3 * VH + (size_t) VLAYERS * SM_N;
    CK(cudaHostAlloc((void**) &g_vis.wb, nb * 2, cudaHostAllocDefault));
    CK(cudaHostAlloc((void**) &g_vis.ws, g_vis.ns * 4, cudaHostAllocDefault));
    g_vis.nb = nb;
    for (auto& m : mats) {
        const auto b = E.ck->bytes(m.first);
        std::memcpy(g_vis.wb + *m.second, b.data(), b.size());
    }
    size_t o = 0;
    auto put = [&](const std::string& name, size_t want) {
        const vec v = E.ck->f32(name);
        if (v.size() != want) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s: %zu values, expected %zu\n", name.c_str(), v.size(), want); std::exit(1); }
        std::memcpy(g_vis.ws + o, v.data(), want * 4);
        o += want;
    };
    g_vis.s_pos = o; put(B + "embeddings.position_embedding", (size_t) VGRID * VGRID * VH);
    g_vis.s_cls = o; put(B + "embeddings.class_embedding", VH);
    {   // the class token's input: class_embedding + cls_position_embedding
        const vec c = E.ck->f32(B + "embeddings.cls_position_embedding");
        for (int i = 0; i < VH; ++i) g_vis.ws[g_vis.s_cls + i] += c[i];
    }
    g_vis.s_lnw = o; put(B + "ln_pre.weight", VH);
    g_vis.s_lnb = o; put(B + "ln_pre.bias", VH);
    const char* sm[12] = {"self_attn.q_proj.bias", "self_attn.k_proj.bias", "self_attn.v_proj.bias", "self_attn.out_proj.bias",
                          "mlp.c_fc.bias", "mlp.c_proj.bias", "ln_1.weight", "ln_1.bias", "ln_2.weight", "ln_2.bias",
                          "ls_1.gamma", "ls_2.gamma"};
    for (int l = 0; l < VLAYERS; ++l) {
        g_vis.s_l[l] = o;
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; ++j) put(B + "layers." + std::to_string(l) + "." + sm[j], j == 4 ? VFF : VH);
    }
    CK(cublasCreate(&g_vis.cb) == CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS ? cudaSuccess : cudaErrorUnknown);
    cublasSetStream(g_vis.cb, cudaStreamPerThread);
    cublasSetMathMode(g_vis.cb, CUBLAS_PEDANTIC_MATH);
    g_vis.loaded = true;
    std::fprintf(stderr, "[vision] weights read: %.0f MB BF16 + %.1f MB fp32 in pinned host memory, %.2f s\n", nb * 2 / 1e6,
                 g_vis.ns * 4 / 1e6, (vis_ms() - t0) / 1000);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- kernels
__global__ void kv_bf16_f32(const uint16_t* __restrict__ s, float* __restrict__ d, size_t n) {
    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t) blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n; i += (size_t) gridDim.x * blockDim.x)
        d[i] = __uint_as_float((uint32_t) s[i] << 16);
}
__device__ __forceinline__ float kv_block_sum(float v, float* sh) {   // blockDim 256
    v = warp_sum(v);
    if ((threadIdx.x & 31) == 0) sh[threadIdx.x >> 5] = v;
    __syncthreads();
    float t = threadIdx.x < 8 ? sh[threadIdx.x] : 0.0f;
    if (threadIdx.x < 32) t = warp_sum(t);
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) sh[8] = t;
    __syncthreads();
    const float r = sh[8];
    __syncthreads();
    return r;
}
// LayerNorm over rows of 1024, block 256
__global__ void kv_ln(const float* __restrict__ x, const float* __restrict__ w, const float* __restrict__ b, float* __restrict__ y) {
    __shared__ float sh[9];
    const float* r = x + (size_t) blockIdx.x * VH;
    float v[4], s = 0.0f;
    for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) { v[j] = r[threadIdx.x + 256 * j]; s += v[j]; }
    const float mean = kv_block_sum(s, sh) / VH;
    float q = 0.0f;
    for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) { v[j] -= mean; q += v[j] * v[j]; }
    const float inv = rsqrtf(kv_block_sum(q, sh) / VH + VEPS);
    float* o = y + (size_t) blockIdx.x * VH;
    for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) { const int i = threadIdx.x + 256 * j; o[i] = v[j] * inv * w[i] + b[i]; }
}
// softmax of rows of n, block 256
__global__ void kv_softmax(float* __restrict__ s, int n) {
    __shared__ float sh[9];
    float* r = s + (size_t) blockIdx.x * n;
    float m = -INFINITY;
    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += 256) m = fmaxf(m, r[i]);
    m = warp_max(m);
    if ((threadIdx.x & 31) == 0) sh[threadIdx.x >> 5] = m;
    __syncthreads();
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) { float t = sh[0]; for (int k = 1; k < 8; ++k) t = fmaxf(t, sh[k]); sh[8] = t; }
    __syncthreads();
    m = sh[8];
    __syncthreads();
    float z = 0.0f;
    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += 256) { const float e = expf(r[i] - m); r[i] = e; z += e; }
    const float inv = 1.0f / kv_block_sum(z, sh);
    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += 256) r[i] *= inv;
}
// (row, col) of native patch i (block order: 2 x 2 per token, tokens in raster order over `cols` tokens a row)
__device__ __forceinline__ void kv_patch_rc(int i, int cols, int& row, int& col) {
    const int t = i >> 2;
    row = (t / cols) * 2 + ((i >> 1) & 1);
    col = (t % cols) * 2 + (i & 1);
}
// one axis of the position table's bilinear resampling (align_corners False, edge clamp), as transformers'
// _interpolation_axis_taps_weights
__device__ __forceinline__ void kv_taps(int idx, int size, int& t0, int& t1, float& w0, float& w1) {
    const float src = ((float) idx + 0.5f) * (float) VGRID / (float) size - 0.5f, f = floorf(src);
    t0 = min(max((int) f, 0), VGRID - 1);
    t1 = min(max((int) f + 1, 0), VGRID - 1);
    w0 = fmaxf(1.0f - fabsf(src - f), 0.0f);
    w1 = fmaxf(1.0f - fabsf(src - f - 1.0f), 0.0f);
}
// x[0] = class token; x[1 + i] += position embedding of patch i.  grid N + 1, block 256
__global__ void kv_embed(float* __restrict__ x, const float* __restrict__ pos, const float* __restrict__ cls, int rows, int cols) {
    if (blockIdx.x == 0) {
        for (int d = threadIdx.x; d < VH; d += 256) x[d] = cls[d];
        return;
    }
    int r, c, h0, h1, w0i, w1i;
    float hw0, hw1, ww0, ww1;
    kv_patch_rc(blockIdx.x - 1, cols, r, c);
    kv_taps(r, 2 * rows, h0, h1, hw0, hw1);
    kv_taps(c, 2 * cols, w0i, w1i, ww0, ww1);
    const float* p00 = pos + (size_t) (h0 * VGRID + w0i) * VH;
    const float* p01 = pos + (size_t) (h0 * VGRID + w1i) * VH;
    const float* p10 = pos + (size_t) (h1 * VGRID + w0i) * VH;
    const float* p11 = pos + (size_t) (h1 * VGRID + w1i) * VH;
    float* o = x + (size_t) blockIdx.x * VH;
    for (int d = threadIdx.x; d < VH; d += 256)
        o[d] += p00[d] * (hw0 * ww0) + p01[d] * (hw0 * ww1) + p10[d] * (hw1 * ww0) + p11[d] * (hw1 * ww1);
}
// q, k += bias, then the 2D RoPE (interleaved pairs; pair j < 16: column, j >= 16: row; class token at (0, 0)).
// grid S, block 512 (16 heads x 32 pairs)
__global__ void kv_qk_rope(float* __restrict__ q, float* __restrict__ k, float* __restrict__ v, const float* __restrict__ bq,
                           const float* __restrict__ bk, const float* __restrict__ bv, int cols) {
    const int s = blockIdx.x, hd = threadIdx.x >> 5, j = threadIdx.x & 31;
    int r = -1, c = -1;
    if (s > 0) kv_patch_rc(s - 1, cols, r, c);
    const float p = (float) (j < 16 ? c + 1 : r + 1);
    const int jj = j & 15;
    const float inv = 1.0f / powf(10000.0f, (float) (2 * jj) / 32.0f);
    const float a = p * inv, cs = cosf(a), sn = sinf(a);
    const size_t i = (size_t) s * VH + hd * VHD + 2 * j;
    float x0 = q[i] + bq[hd * VHD + 2 * j], x1 = q[i + 1] + bq[hd * VHD + 2 * j + 1];
    q[i] = x0 * cs - x1 * sn;
    q[i + 1] = x1 * cs + x0 * sn;
    x0 = k[i] + bk[hd * VHD + 2 * j];
    x1 = k[i + 1] + bk[hd * VHD + 2 * j + 1];
    k[i] = x0 * cs - x1 * sn;
    k[i + 1] = x1 * cs + x0 * sn;
    v[i] += bv[hd * VHD + 2 * j];
    v[i + 1] += bv[hd * VHD + 2 * j + 1];
}
// x += ls * (y + b), rows of 1024
__global__ void kv_resid(float* __restrict__ x, const float* __restrict__ y, const float* __restrict__ b,
                         const float* __restrict__ ls, size_t n) {
    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t) blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n; i += (size_t) gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {
        const int d = (int) (i % VH);
        x[i] += ls[d] * (y[i] + b[d]);
    }
}
// y = gelu(y + b) (exact erf form), rows of `w` (b may be null)
__global__ void kv_gelu(float* __restrict__ y, const float* __restrict__ b, int w, size_t n) {
    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t) blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n; i += (size_t) gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {
        const float v = y[i] + (b ? b[i % w] : 0.0f);
        y[i] = 0.5f * v * (1.0f + erff(v * 0.70710678118654752f));
    }
}
unsigned kv_grid(size_t n) { return (unsigned) std::min<size_t>((n + 255) / 256, 8192); }

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- the encode's VRAM
struct VisArena { uint8_t* p = nullptr; size_t bytes = 0; int pool_saved = -1; };
void vis_graphs_drop() {   // the decode graph and the verify graphs bake the slot counts in (as pool_resize)
    if (E.exec) { CK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(E.exec)); E.exec = nullptr; }
    for (auto& by_output : g_vexec) for (auto& x : by_output) if (x) { CK(cudaGraphExecDestroy(x)); x = nullptr; }
}
std::string vis_arena_get(VisArena& a, size_t bytes) {
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    a.bytes = bytes;
    if (E.offload) {   // the NVFP4 pool's last slots, evicted (as pool_resize shrinking, without giving the memory up)
        const size_t eb = ex_bytes(0);
        const int k = (int) ((bytes + eb - 1) / eb), ns = E.nslot_nv, n = ns - k;
        if (n < 2 * FETCH_MAX)
            return "the picture needs " + std::to_string(bytes >> 20) + " MiB of VRAM for its encoder, more than the expert pool can lend";
        if (E.cpu) promote_cancel();
        k_pool_evict<<<(k + 255) / 256, 256>>>(E.owner_nv, E.slot_of, E.tabA, E.tabB, E.stamp_nv, n, ns);
        CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
        a.pool_saved = ns;
        E.nslot_nv = n;
        vis_graphs_drop();
        a.p = E.pool_nv + (size_t) n * eb;
        std::fprintf(stderr, "[vision] %zu MiB lent by the expert pool (%d NVFP4 slots)\n", bytes >> 20, k);
        return "";
    }
    const size_t fr = vram_free(), keep = vram_keep();
    if (fr < bytes + keep || cudaMalloc((void**) &a.p, bytes) != cudaSuccess) {
        (void) cudaGetLastError();
        a.p = nullptr;
        return "the picture needs " + std::to_string(bytes >> 20) + " MiB of VRAM for its encoder, " +
               std::to_string(fr > keep ? (fr - keep) >> 20 : 0) + " MiB are free";
    }
    return "";
}
void vis_arena_put(VisArena& a) {
    CK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
    if (a.pool_saved >= 0) {   // the slots come back empty (owner -1, stamp 0): the pool fills them on demand
        E.nslot_nv = a.pool_saved;
        vis_graphs_drop();
    } else if (a.p) {
        CK(cudaFree(a.p));
    }
    a = VisArena();
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- the ViT
// one picture -> its rows x cols image rows [n][H] (host)
std::string vis_run(const ink2img::Picture& pic, float* out) {
    vis_load();
    const int NT = pic.rows * pic.cols, N = 4 * NT, S = N + 1;
    const size_t SH = (size_t) S * VH, WMAX = (size_t) VPROJ * VMERGE_IN, WS_BYTES = (size_t) 4 << 20;
    auto al = [](size_t b) { return (b + 255) / 256 * 256; };
    const size_t b_small = al(g_vis.ns * 4), b_stage = al(WMAX * 2), b_wf = al(WMAX * 4), b_act = al((5 * SH + VSC) * 4);
    VisArena A;
    const size_t free0 = vram_free();
    std::string err = vis_arena_get(A, b_small + b_stage + b_wf + WS_BYTES + b_act);
    if (!err.empty()) return err;
    const double t0 = vis_ms();
    uint8_t* p = A.p;
    float* sm = (float*) p;           p += b_small;
    uint16_t* stg = (uint16_t*) p;    p += b_stage;
    float* wf = (float*) p;           p += b_wf;
    void* cws = p;                    p += WS_BYTES;
    float* x = (float*) p;
    float *y = x + SH, *q = y + SH, *k = q + SH, *v = k + SH, *sc = v + SH;
    cublasSetWorkspace(g_vis.cb, cws, WS_BYTES);
    const cudaStream_t st = cudaStreamPerThread;
    CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(sm, g_vis.ws, g_vis.ns * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st));
    size_t h2d = g_vis.ns * 4;
    auto wmat = [&](size_t off, size_t n, float* dst) {   // a BF16 matrix -> fp32 at dst
        CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(stg, g_vis.wb + off, n * 2, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st));
        kv_bf16_f32<<<kv_grid(n), 256, 0, st>>>(stg, dst, n);
        h2d += n * 2;
    };
    const float one = 1.0f, zero = 0.0f;
    bool ok = true;
    // Y[T][n] = X[T][K] . W[n][K]^T (row-major; ldw: W's row stride)
    auto gemm = [&](int T, int n, int K, const float* X, int ldx, const float* W, int ldw, float* Y, int ldy, float alpha) {
        if (cublasSgemm(g_vis.cb, CUBLAS_OP_T, CUBLAS_OP_N, n, T, K, &alpha, W, ldw, X, ldx, &zero, Y, ldy) != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS)
            ok = false;
    };
    // the patches -> x[1..N], the class token at x[0], the position table, ln_pre
    CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(k, pic.patches.data(), (size_t) N * ink2img::PIN * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, st));
    h2d += (size_t) N * ink2img::PIN * 4;
    wmat(g_vis.m_patch, (size_t) VH * ink2img::PIN, wf);
    gemm(N, VH, ink2img::PIN, k, ink2img::PIN, wf, ink2img::PIN, x + VH, VH, one);
    kv_embed<<<S, 256, 0, st>>>(x, sm + g_vis.s_pos, sm + g_vis.s_cls, pic.rows, pic.cols);
    kv_ln<<<S, 256, 0, st>>>(x, sm + g_vis.s_lnw, sm + g_vis.s_lnb, y);
    std::swap(x, y);
    const int qc_max = (int) std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(S, VSC / S)), mc = (int) (VSC / VFF);
    const size_t M1 = (size_t) VH * VH, M4 = (size_t) VFF * VH;
    for (int l = 0; l < VLAYERS; ++l) {
        const float* s = sm + g_vis.s_l[l];
        float *wq = wf, *wk = wf + M1, *wv = wf + 2 * M1, *wo = wf + 3 * M1, *w1 = wf + 4 * M1, *w2 = w1 + M4;
        wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][0], M1, wq); wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][1], M1, wk); wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][2], M1, wv);
        wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][3], M1, wo); wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][4], M4, w1); wmat(g_vis.m_l[l][5], M4, w2);
        kv_ln<<<S, 256, 0, st>>>(x, s + SM_LN1W, s + SM_LN1B, y);
        gemm(S, VH, VH, y, VH, wq, VH, q, VH, one);
        gemm(S, VH, VH, y, VH, wk, VH, k, VH, one);
        gemm(S, VH, VH, y, VH, wv, VH, v, VH, one);
        kv_qk_rope<<<S, 512, 0, st>>>(q, k, v, s + SM_BQ, s + SM_BK, s + SM_BV, pic.cols);
        for (int h = 0; h < VHEADS; ++h)   // attention -> y (the heads' outputs side by side)
            for (int q0 = 0; q0 < S; q0 += qc_max) {
                const int qc = std::min(qc_max, S - q0);
                gemm(qc, S, VHD, q + (size_t) q0 * VH + h * VHD, VH, k + h * VHD, VH, sc, S, 0.125f);   // 1 / sqrt(64)
                kv_softmax<<<qc, 256, 0, st>>>(sc, S);
                if (cublasSgemm(g_vis.cb, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N, VHD, qc, S, &one, v + h * VHD, VH, sc, S, &zero,
                                y + (size_t) q0 * VH + h * VHD, VH) != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) ok = false;
            }
        gemm(S, VH, VH, y, VH, wo, VH, q, VH, one);
        kv_resid<<<kv_grid(SH), 256, 0, st>>>(x, q, s + SM_BO, s + SM_LS1, SH);
        kv_ln<<<S, 256, 0, st>>>(x, s + SM_LN2W, s + SM_LN2B, y);
        for (int r0 = 0; r0 < S; r0 += mc) {
            const int m = std::min(mc, S - r0);
            gemm(m, VFF, VH, y + (size_t) r0 * VH, VH, w1, VH, sc, VFF, one);
            kv_gelu<<<kv_grid((size_t) m * VFF), 256, 0, st>>>(sc, s + SM_B1, VFF, (size_t) m * VFF);
            gemm(m, VH, VFF, sc, VFF, w2, VFF, q + (size_t) r0 * VH, VH, one);
        }
        kv_resid<<<kv_grid(SH), 256, 0, st>>>(x, q, s + SM_B2, s + SM_LS2, SH);
    }
    // projector: the 4 patch rows of a token side by side are x[1 + 4t .. 1 + 4t + 3] (block order), already contiguous
    wmat(g_vis.m_in, (size_t) VPROJ * VMERGE_IN, wf);
    gemm(NT, VPROJ, VMERGE_IN, x + VH, VMERGE_IN, wf, VMERGE_IN, q, VPROJ, one);
    kv_gelu<<<kv_grid((size_t) NT * VPROJ), 256, 0, st>>>(q, nullptr, VPROJ, (size_t) NT * VPROJ);
    wmat(g_vis.m_out, (size_t) H * VPROJ, wf);
    gemm(NT, H, VPROJ, q, VPROJ, wf, VPROJ, k, H, one);
    CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(out, k, (size_t) NT * H * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, st));
    CK(cudaStreamSynchronize(st));
    const double t1 = vis_ms();
    const size_t arena = A.bytes;
    vis_arena_put(A);
    if (!ok) return "cuBLAS failed in the picture encoder";
    const size_t free1 = vram_free();
    std::fprintf(stderr, "[vision] %dx%d px (decoded %dx%d) -> %d x %d image tokens, ViT %d tokens: %.0f ms, %.0f MiB VRAM, "
                         "%.0f MB weights over PCIe; VRAM free %.0f MiB before, %.0f after\n", pic.w, pic.h, pic.src_w, pic.src_h,
                 pic.rows, pic.cols, S, t1 - t0, arena / 1048576.0, h2d / 1e6, free0 / 1048576.0, free1 / 1048576.0);
    return "";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- the prompt
// the prompt id of image token j of the picture with key k: negative (never a vocabulary id), 31 bits of a mix of both
// CBA: two different pictures match only if all 31-bit ids of the shared span collide (~2^-31 per token)
int vis_id(uint64_t k, int j) {
    uint64_t x = k ^ (0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ull * (uint64_t) (j + 1));
    x ^= x >> 33; x *= 0xff51afd7ed558ccdull; x ^= x >> 33; x *= 0xc4ceb9fe1a85ec53ull; x ^= x >> 33;
    return -1 - (int) (x & 0x3fffffff);
}
// This prompt's pictures: each IMAGE_TOKEN of `ids` (one per picture, prompt order) becomes its rows x cols image
// tokens.  `paths` empty and no placeholder: a text prompt (nothing to do).  "" or the error.
std::string vis_prepare(std::vector<int>& ids, const std::vector<std::string>& paths, bool pixels = false) {
    g_vis.spans.clear();
    g_vis.pics.clear();
    g_vis.pending = false;
    const size_t nph = (size_t) std::count(ids.begin(), ids.end(), IMAGE_TOKEN);
    if (paths.empty() && nph == 0) return "";
    if (!vis_available()) return "this model has no image encoder (model.visual.*; a GGUF file is text only here)";
    if (nph != paths.size())
        return std::to_string(paths.size()) + " picture(s) for " + std::to_string(nph) + " image placeholder(s) in the prompt";
    g_vis.pics.resize(paths.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < paths.size(); ++i) {
        const std::string e = pixels ? ink2img::load_pixels(paths[i], g_vis.pics[i])
                                     : ink2img::load_picture(paths[i], g_vis.max_tokens, g_vis.pics[i]);
        if (!e.empty()) return e;
    }
    std::vector<int> out;
    out.reserve(ids.size());
    size_t k = 0, row = 0;
    for (int t : ids) {
        if (t != IMAGE_TOKEN) { out.push_back(t); continue; }
        const ink2img::Picture& p = g_vis.pics[k++];
        const int n = p.rows * p.cols;
        g_vis.spans.push_back({(int) out.size(), n, p.key, row});
        row += (size_t) n;
        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) out.push_back(vis_id(p.key, j));
    }
    ids.swap(out);
    return "";
}

// Encodes the pictures whose rows are read from `start` on (the others are in the reused prefix).  "" or the error.
std::string vis_encode(size_t start) {
    if (g_vis.spans.empty()) return "";
    size_t rows = 0;
    for (const auto& s : g_vis.spans) rows += (size_t) s.n;
    if (rows > g_vis.emb_cap) {
        if (g_vis.emb) CK(cudaFreeHost(g_vis.emb));
        CK(cudaHostAlloc((void**) &g_vis.emb, rows * H * 4, cudaHostAllocDefault));
        g_vis.emb_cap = rows;
    }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < g_vis.spans.size(); ++i) {
        const Vis::Span& s = g_vis.spans[i];
        if ((size_t) (s.pos + s.n) <= start) continue;
        const std::string e = vis_run(g_vis.pics[i], g_vis.emb + s.row * H);
        if (!e.empty()) return e;
    }
    g_vis.pics.clear();   // (the patches: up to tens of MB a picture)
    if (!g_vis.dump.empty()) {
        if (FILE* f = std::fopen(g_vis.dump.c_str(), "wb")) {
            std::fwrite(g_vis.emb, 4, rows * H, f);
            std::fclose(f);
        }
    }
    g_vis.pending = true;
    return "";
}

// forward_prefill_device: the chunk [pos0, pos0 + T)'s image rows over its normed embeddings (PF.h)
void vis_inject(int T, int pos0) {
    if (!g_vis.pending || pos0 < 0) return;
    for (const auto& s : g_vis.spans) {
        const int a = std::max(pos0, s.pos), b = std::min(pos0 + T, s.pos + s.n);
        if (a >= b) continue;
        CK(cudaMemcpyAsync(PF.h + (size_t) (a - pos0) * H, g_vis.emb + (s.row + (size_t) (a - s.pos)) * H,
                           (size_t) (b - a) * H * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, cudaStreamPerThread));
    }
}

// --vis-pixels FILE: the ViT alone on the processor's pixel tensor (no language model loaded), rows to --dump-image-emb
int vis_cli_pixels(const std::string& path) {
    std::vector<int> ids = {IMAGE_TOKEN};
    std::string e = vis_prepare(ids, {path}, true);
    if (e.empty()) e = vis_encode(0);
    if (!e.empty()) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", e.c_str()); return 1; }
    std::printf("vision: %zu image rows%s%s\n", ids.size(), g_vis.dump.empty() ? "" : " written to ", g_vis.dump.c_str());
    return 0;
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2_vision.cuh =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2ref.cu =====
// src/inkling2/ink2ref.cu - NEW-MODELS-PLAN.md phase T1 for Inkling-2-Mini (thinkingmachines/Inkling-2-Mini,
// model_type inkling2_loop): the reference path, exactness first.  One GPU, every weight resident in VRAM in its
// checkpoint form (BF16 dense, NVFP4 experts on layers 0-19, MXFP8 experts on layers 20-23: ~18 GiB), one token at a
// time (the prompt too), fp32 activations.  The GPU only runs the matrix-vector products (plain kernels of this file);
// everything else is host fp32, written to mirror the transformers reference (PR #51050,
// models/inkling2_loop/modeling_inkling2_loop.py) line for line, so a mismatch points at one operation.
// No expert cache, no batching, no MTP, no vision.  Checked against that reference by tools/inkling2/ (per layer
