// application outputs, logits, greedy tokens).
//
// CBA: host-side attention and convolutions, one cudaMemcpy pair per GEMV - a reference, ~0.1-0.3 s per token; the
// engine path (T2) moves all of it onto the GPU.
//
// The forward pass (per token):
//   h = rms(embed[tok]) * embed_norm;  B = []                       (B: "block states", snapshots of h)
//   layers 0-5, then the loop layers 6-17 (pass 0), then passes 1 and 2 of 6-17 restarted from the pre-loop (h, B) and
//   fed "messages" from the previous pass, then layers 18-23;  a layer whose index is a multiple of 6 appends its
//   INPUT to B.  Each pass of a loop layer has its own KV cache and convolution states (48 slots in all).
//   h = attnres(final_query, B, h); logits = softcap30(unembed (rms(h) * norm))
// One layer L (full attention when L % 6 == 5, else sliding window 1024):
//   a = attnres(attention_input[L], B, h)  (absent for L 0; three queries q/k/v on full layers)
//   [loop passes 1-2: a = message_gate(a, message)]   x = rms(a) * norm   (q/k/v norms on full layers)
//   att = attention(xq, xk, xv); att = shortconv(att)
//   r = (L % 6 == 0) ? att : h + att                (on a block boundary the residual restarts; B gets h)
//   m = attnres(mlp_input[L], B, r) [loop passes 1-2: message_gate(m, message)]
//   out = r + shortconv(MoE(rms(m) * mlp_norm))
//
// Usage: bel-inkling2-ref --model DIR (--tokens 1,2,3 | --tokens-file F) [--n 16] [--dump-layers FILE] [--dump-logits FILE]
//                       [--pos-offset N]
//   --dump-layers writes every position's 48 layer-application outputs (f32, [pos][48][2304]); --dump-logits every
//   position's logits (f32, [pos][200058]); prints the greedy continuation ("gen: id id ...").
#include "safetensors_lite.hpp"

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <numeric>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace {

using vec = std::vector<float>;
using bel::safetensors::SafeTensorFile;
using bel::safetensors::SafeTensor;

// config.json (text_config) of Inkling-2-Mini.  CBA: fixed geometry, read from config.json if a second size ships.
constexpr int H = 2304, NL = 24, NQ = 24, NKV = 8, HD = 128, NREP = NQ / NKV, DREL = 16, EXTENT = 1024, WINDOW = 1024;
constexpr int NE = 128, TOPK = 8, FF = 1152, KC = 4, VOCAB = 200058;
constexpr int LOOP_BEGIN = 6, LOOP_END = 18, NLOOP = LOOP_END - LOOP_BEGIN, PASSES = 3, BLOCK = 6;
constexpr int NCALLS = NL + (PASSES - 1) * NLOOP;   // 48 layer applications
constexpr int MXFP8_FIRST = 20;                      // hf_quant_config.json: experts of layers 20-23 are MXFP8
constexpr float EPS = 1e-6f, QK_CLIP = 22.627416997969522f, REL_CLIP = 8.0f, LOG_ALPHA = 0.1f, LOG_FLOOR = 8192.0f;
constexpr float GATE_CAP = 4.0f, UP_CAP = 25.0f, ROUTE_SCALE = 8.0f, LOGIT_CAP = 30.0f;

bool full_layer(int L) { return L % BLOCK == BLOCK - 1; }   // local_layer_ids omit 5, 11, 17, 23

#define CK(x) do { cudaError_t e_ = (x); if (e_ != cudaSuccess) { \
    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA %s at %s:%d\n", cudaGetErrorString(e_), __FILE__, __LINE__); std::exit(1); } } while (0)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GPU: the matrix-vector products, one warp per output row, fp32 accumulation.

__device__ __forceinline__ float bf16f(uint16_t b) { return __uint_as_float(((uint32_t) b) << 16); }

__device__ __forceinline__ float e4m3f(uint8_t v) {   // float8_e4m3fn: bias 7, no infinities, S.1111.111 = NaN
    const int e = (v >> 3) & 15, m = v & 7;
    float r = e == 0 ? (float) m * 0.001953125f /* m/8 * 2^-6 */ : ldexpf(1.0f + (float) m * 0.125f, e - 7);
    if (e == 15 && m == 7) r = __int_as_float(0x7fc00000);
    return (v & 0x80) ? -r : r;
}

__constant__ float kE2M1[16] = {0.0f, 0.5f, 1.0f, 1.5f, 2.0f, 3.0f, 4.0f, 6.0f,
                                -0.0f, -0.5f, -1.0f, -1.5f, -2.0f, -3.0f, -4.0f, -6.0f};

__device__ __forceinline__ float warp_sum(float a) {
    for (int o = 16; o; o >>= 1) a += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, a, o);
    return a;
}

__global__ void gemv_bf16(const uint16_t* W, const float* x, float* y, int N, int K) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32, lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    if (row >= N) return;
    const uint16_t* w = W + (size_t) row * K;
    float acc = 0.0f;
    for (int k = lane; k < K; k += 32) acc += bf16f(w[k]) * x[k];
    acc = warp_sum(acc);
    if (!lane) y[row] = acc;
}

// NVFP4 (ModelOpt layout of this checkpoint): byte b of a row holds columns 2b (low nibble) and 2b+1 (high nibble);
// one e4m3 scale per 16 columns; one fp32 factor per expert (amax / outer_scale_denominator), applied to the sum.
__global__ void gemv_nvfp4(const uint8_t* W, const uint8_t* S, float factor, const float* x, float* y, int N, int K) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32, lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    if (row >= N) return;
    const uint8_t* w = W + (size_t) row * (K / 2);
    const uint8_t* s = S + (size_t) row * (K / 16);
    float acc = 0.0f;
    for (int b = lane; b < K / 2; b += 32) {
        const uint8_t v = w[b];
        const float sc = e4m3f(s[b / 8]);
        acc += (kE2M1[v & 15] * sc) * x[2 * b] + (kE2M1[v >> 4] * sc) * x[2 * b + 1];
    }
    acc = warp_sum(acc);
    if (!lane) y[row] = acc * factor;
}

// MXFP8: e4m3 values, one ue8m0 exponent per 32 columns (2^(s - 127)).
__global__ void gemv_mxfp8(const uint8_t* W, const uint8_t* S, const float* x, float* y, int N, int K) {
    const int row = blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x / 32) + threadIdx.x / 32, lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    if (row >= N) return;
    const uint8_t* w = W + (size_t) row * K;
    const uint8_t* s = S + (size_t) row * (K / 32);
    float acc = 0.0f;
    for (int k = lane; k < K; k += 32) acc += (e4m3f(w[k]) * exp2f((float) s[k / 32] - 127.0f)) * x[k];
    acc = warp_sum(acc);
    if (!lane) y[row] = acc;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Checkpoint access

struct Ckpt {
    std::string path;
    SafeTensorFile st;
    const SafeTensor& t(const std::string& name) const {
        auto it = st.tensors.find(name);
        if (it == st.tensors.end()) { std::fprintf(stderr, "missing tensor %s\n", name.c_str()); std::exit(1); }
        return it->second;
    }
    bool has(const std::string& name) const { return st.tensors.count(name) != 0; }
    std::vector<unsigned char> bytes(const std::string& name) const {
        std::vector<unsigned char> b;
        const std::string e = bel::safetensors::read_tensor(path, st, t(name), b);
        if (!e.empty()) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", e.c_str()); std::exit(1); }
        return b;
    }
    vec f32(const std::string& name) const {   // BF16 or F32 -> fp32 (exact)
        const SafeTensor& d = t(name);
        auto b = bytes(name);
        vec out;
        if (d.dtype == "F32") {
            out.resize(b.size() / 4);
            std::memcpy(out.data(), b.data(), b.size());
        } else if (d.dtype == "BF16") {
            out.resize(b.size() / 2);
            for (size_t i = 0; i < out.size(); ++i) {
                uint32_t u = (uint32_t) (b[2 * i] | (b[2 * i + 1] << 8)) << 16;
                std::memcpy(&out[i], &u, 4);
            }
        } else { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s: dtype %s not handled\n", name.c_str(), d.dtype.c_str()); std::exit(1); }
        return out;
    }
};

const Ckpt* g_ck = nullptr;
size_t g_vram = 0;

void* upload(const std::vector<unsigned char>& b) {
    void* d = nullptr;
    CK(cudaMalloc(&d, b.size()));
    CK(cudaMemcpy(d, b.data(), b.size(), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    g_vram += b.size();
    return d;
}

struct DMat {   // a BF16 matrix [n, k] in VRAM
    const uint16_t* d = nullptr;
    int n = 0, k = 0;
};

DMat dmat(const std::string& name) {
    const SafeTensor& t = g_ck->t(name);
    if (t.dtype != "BF16") { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s: expected BF16, got %s\n", name.c_str(), t.dtype.c_str()); std::exit(1); }
    DMat m;
    m.n = (int) t.shape[0];
    m.k = 1;
    for (size_t i = 1; i < t.shape.size(); ++i) m.k *= (int) t.shape[i];
    m.d = (const uint16_t*) upload(g_ck->bytes(name));
    return m;
}

float* g_dx = nullptr;
float* g_dy = nullptr;


vec mv(const DMat& W, const vec& x, int rows = -1) {   // W x, the first `rows` rows (all by default)
    const int n = rows < 0 ? W.n : rows;
    if ((int) x.size() != W.k) { std::fprintf(stderr, "mv: %zu != %d\n", x.size(), W.k); std::exit(1); }
    CK(cudaMemcpy(g_dx, x.data(), x.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    gemv_bf16<<<(n + 7) / 8, 256>>>(W.d, g_dx, g_dy, n, W.k);
    vec y((size_t) n);
    CK(cudaMemcpy(y.data(), g_dy, y.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    return y;
}

struct Experts {
    bool nvfp4 = true;
    const uint8_t *w13 = nullptr, *s13 = nullptr, *w2 = nullptr, *s2 = nullptr;
    vec f13, f2;   // NVFP4: amax / denominator per expert
};

// gate_up rows of expert e: [2*FF rows (gate/up interleaved: row 2j gate j, 2j+1 up j), H columns]; down: [H, FF]
vec expert_mv(const Experts& X, int e, bool up_proj, const vec& x) {
    const int N = up_proj ? 2 * FF : H, K = up_proj ? H : FF;
    CK(cudaMemcpy(g_dx, x.data(), (size_t) K * 4, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    if (X.nvfp4) {
        const uint8_t* w = (up_proj ? X.w13 : X.w2) + (size_t) e * N * (K / 2);
        const uint8_t* s = (up_proj ? X.s13 : X.s2) + (size_t) e * N * (K / 16);
        gemv_nvfp4<<<(N + 7) / 8, 256>>>(w, s, (up_proj ? X.f13 : X.f2)[e], g_dx, g_dy, N, K);
    } else {
        const uint8_t* w = (up_proj ? X.w13 : X.w2) + (size_t) e * N * K;
        const uint8_t* s = (up_proj ? X.s13 : X.s2) + (size_t) e * N * (K / 32);
        gemv_mxfp8<<<(N + 7) / 8, 256>>>(w, s, g_dx, g_dy, N, K);
    }
    vec y((size_t) N);
    CK(cudaMemcpy(y.data(), g_dy, y.size() * 4, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    return y;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Host fp32 operations (each one named after its transformers module)

float dot(const float* a, const float* b, int n) {
    float s = 0.0f;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) s += a[i] * b[i];
    return s;
}

vec rms(const vec& x, const vec* w) {   // Inkling2LoopRMSNorm (w) / Inkling2LoopUnweightedRMSNorm (nullptr)
    double ss = 0.0;   // CBA: double for the mean; torch reduces fp32 pairwise, the difference is ~1e-7 relative
    for (float v : x) ss += (double) v * v;
    const float r = 1.0f / std::sqrt((float) (ss / x.size()) + EPS);
    vec y(x.size());
    for (size_t i = 0; i < x.size(); ++i) y[i] = w ? (*w)[i] * (x[i] * r) : x[i] * r;
    return y;
}

float sigmoidf(float v) { return 1.0f / (1.0f + std::exp(-v)); }

int g_pos_offset = 0;   // --pos-offset: shifts the positions Inkling2LoopLogScale sees (only), to test it past 8191

float log_scale(int pos) {   // Inkling2LoopLogScale
    pos += g_pos_offset;
    return 1.0f + LOG_ALPHA * std::log(std::max((float) (pos + 1) / LOG_FLOOR, 1.0f));
}

void l2_clip(float* v, int n, float thr) {   // Inkling2LoopL2Clip
    const float norm = std::sqrt(dot(v, v, n));
    const float s = std::min(thr / (norm + 1e-6f), 1.0f);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) v[i] *= s;
}

// Inkling2LoopBlockAttentionResidual / MessageAttentionResidual: softmax over the normalized stack [B..., h] of
// (q . s_j) / sqrt(H), returns sum_j p_j s_j (the NORMALIZED states), one output per query.
std::vector<vec> attnres(const float* queries, int nq, const std::vector<vec>& B, const vec& h) {
    std::vector<vec> s;
    for (const vec& b : B) s.push_back(rms(b, nullptr));
    s.push_back(rms(h, nullptr));
    std::vector<vec> out;
    const float scale = 1.0f / std::sqrt((float) H);
    for (int q = 0; q < nq; ++q) {
        vec lg(s.size());
        for (size_t j = 0; j < s.size(); ++j) lg[j] = dot(queries + (size_t) q * H, s[j].data(), H) * scale;
        const float mx = *std::max_element(lg.begin(), lg.end());
        float z = 0.0f;
        for (float& v : lg) { v = std::exp(v - mx); z += v; }
        vec o(H, 0.0f);
        for (size_t j = 0; j < s.size(); ++j)
            for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) o[i] += (lg[j] / z) * s[j][i];
        out.push_back(o);
    }
    return out;
}

struct MsgGate {   // Inkling2LoopMessageGate (checkpoint: controllers.{attn,k,v,mlp}.{down,forget,gain}_weight)
    DMat down, forget, gain;
};

vec message_gate(const MsgGate& g, const vec& x, const vec& m) {   // (1 - tanh(F d)) * x + tanh(G d) * m
    vec cat = rms(x, nullptr);
    vec mn = rms(m, nullptr);
    cat.insert(cat.end(), mn.begin(), mn.end());
    const vec d = mv(g.down, cat);
    const vec f = mv(g.forget, d), gn = mv(g.gain, d);
    vec y(H);
    for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) y[i] = (1.0f - std::tanh(f[i])) * x[i] + std::tanh(gn[i]) * m[i];
    return y;
}

// Inkling2LoopShortConvolution: depthwise causal conv over the sequence (taps w[c][0..3], w[c][3] on the current
// token), out = (conv + x) / sqrt(5).  `hist` holds this slot's previous inputs, oldest first.
vec short_conv(const vec& x, std::vector<vec>& hist, const vec& w) {
    const int C = (int) x.size();
    vec y(C);
    for (int c = 0; c < C; ++c) {
        float acc = w[(size_t) c * KC + KC - 1] * x[c];
        for (int j = 1; j < KC; ++j) {
            const int back = (int) hist.size() - j;
            if (back >= 0) acc += w[(size_t) c * KC + KC - 1 - j] * hist[back][c];
        }
        y[c] = (acc + x[c]) / std::sqrt((float) (KC + 1));
    }
    hist.push_back(x);
    return y;
}

// Inkling2LoopDynamicShortConvolution (full layers' keys and values): taps = static_w[lag][c] + (Wp xin)[head*4+lag],
// out = (sum_lag taps * x[t - lag] + x) / sqrt(9).
vec dyn_conv(const vec& x, const vec& xin, std::vector<vec>& hist, const vec& static_w, const DMat& wproj) {
    const vec dw = mv(wproj, xin);   // [NKV * KC]
    const int C = (int) x.size(), per_head = C / NKV;
    vec y(C);
    for (int c = 0; c < C; ++c) {
        float acc = 0.0f;
        for (int lag = 0; lag < KC; ++lag) {
            const float tap = static_w[(size_t) lag * C + c] + dw[(c / per_head) * KC + lag];
            const float* past = lag == 0 ? x.data() : ((int) hist.size() - lag >= 0 ? hist[hist.size() - lag].data() : nullptr);
            if (past) acc += tap * past[c];
        }
        y[c] = (acc + x[c]) / std::sqrt((float) (2 * KC + 1));
    }
    hist.push_back(x);
    return y;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The model

struct Layer {
    bool full = false, loop = false;
    DMat wq, wk, wv, wo, gate_attn, wr, sink, router;
    DMat k_wproj, v_wproj;                 // full layers
    vec q_scale, rel_scale, rel_proj;      // [24*128], [24*16], [16*1024]
    vec k_conv, v_conv, k_static, v_static, attn_conv, mlp_conv;
    vec attn_norm, q_norm, k_norm, v_norm, mlp_norm;
    vec attn_query, mlp_query;             // attention_input (1 or 3 queries; empty on layer 0), mlp_input
    vec router_bias;
    Experts ex;
    MsgGate gates[4];                      // q, k, v, mlp (loop layers)
};

struct Slot {                              // one layer application's state across positions
    std::vector<vec> K, V;                 // [pos][NKV*HD] after the clip / norm
    std::vector<vec> khist, vhist, ahist, mhist;
};

struct Model {
    vec embed_norm, norm, final_query, msg_queries;   // msg_queries: [NLOOP][4 (q,k,v,mlp)][H]
    std::vector<uint16_t> embed;                     // host BF16 [201024 * H]
    DMat unembed;
    Layer L[NL];
    Slot slots[NCALLS];
};

Model* M = nullptr;

void load(const Ckpt& ck) {
    M = new Model();
    const std::string p = "model.llm.";
    M->embed_norm = ck.f32(p + "embed_norm.weight");
    M->norm = ck.f32(p + "norm.weight");
    M->final_query = ck.f32(p + "attn_res.output_query");
    {
        auto b = ck.bytes(p + "embed.weight");
        M->embed.resize(b.size() / 2);
        std::memcpy(M->embed.data(), b.data(), b.size());
    }
    M->unembed = dmat(p + "unembed.weight");
    {   // Inkling2LoopMergeMessageQueries: queries rows (2i, 2i+1) = (q, mlp), kv_queries rows (2i, 2i+1) = (k, v)
        const vec q = ck.f32(p + "middle_cycle_cross_pass_queries"), kv = ck.f32(p + "middle_cycle_cross_pass_kv_queries");
        M->msg_queries.resize((size_t) NLOOP * 4 * H);
        for (int i = 0; i < NLOOP; ++i) {
            const float* src[4] = {&q[(size_t) (2 * i) * H], &kv[(size_t) (2 * i) * H], &kv[(size_t) (2 * i + 1) * H],
                                   &q[(size_t) (2 * i + 1) * H]};
            for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j) std::memcpy(&M->msg_queries[((size_t) i * 4 + j) * H], src[j], H * 4);
        }
    }
    for (int l = 0; l < NL; ++l) {
        Layer& Y = M->L[l];
        const std::string q = p + "layers." + std::to_string(l) + ".";
        Y.full = full_layer(l);
        Y.loop = l >= LOOP_BEGIN && l < LOOP_END;
        Y.wq = dmat(q + "attn.wq_du.weight");
        Y.wk = dmat(q + "attn.wk_dv.weight");
        Y.wv = dmat(q + "attn.wv_dv.weight");
        Y.wo = dmat(q + "attn.wo_ud.weight");
        Y.gate_attn = dmat(q + "attn.gate_attn.weight");
        Y.wr = dmat(q + "attn.wr_du.weight");
        Y.sink = dmat(q + "attn.attn_sink_proj.weight");
        Y.router = dmat(q + "mlp.gate.weight");
        Y.router_bias = ck.f32(q + "mlp.gate.bias");
        Y.q_scale = ck.f32(q + "attn.q_l2_clip.scale");
        Y.rel_scale = ck.f32(q + "attn.rel_l2_clip.scale");
        Y.rel_proj = ck.f32(q + "attn.rel_logits_proj.proj");
        Y.attn_conv = ck.f32(q + "attn_sconv.weight");
        Y.mlp_conv = ck.f32(q + "mlp_sconv.weight");
        Y.mlp_norm = ck.f32(q + "mlp_norm.weight");
        if (Y.full) {
            Y.k_static = ck.f32(q + "attn.k_sconv.static_w");
            Y.v_static = ck.f32(q + "attn.v_sconv.static_w");
            Y.k_wproj = dmat(q + "attn.k_sconv.weight_proj");
            Y.v_wproj = dmat(q + "attn.v_sconv.weight_proj");
            Y.q_norm = ck.f32(q + "q_input_norm.weight");
            Y.k_norm = ck.f32(q + "k_input_norm.weight");
            Y.v_norm = ck.f32(q + "v_input_norm.weight");
        } else {
            Y.k_conv = ck.f32(q + "attn.k_sconv.weight");
            Y.v_conv = ck.f32(q + "attn.v_sconv.weight");
            Y.attn_norm = ck.f32(q + "attn_norm.weight");
        }
        if (l > 0) Y.attn_query = ck.f32(p + "attn_res.attn_queries." + std::to_string(l - 1));   // offset 1
        Y.mlp_query = ck.f32(p + "attn_res.mlp_queries." + std::to_string(l));
        if ((int) Y.attn_query.size() != (l == 0 ? 0 : (Y.full ? 3 : 1)) * H) {
            std::fprintf(stderr, "layer %d: attention_input query size %zu\n", l, Y.attn_query.size());
            std::exit(1);
        }
        if (Y.loop) {
            const char* names[4] = {"attn", "k", "v", "mlp"};
            for (int g = 0; g < 4; ++g) {
                const std::string c = q + "controllers." + names[g] + ".";
                Y.gates[g] = {dmat(c + "down_weight"), dmat(c + "forget_weight"), dmat(c + "gain_weight")};
            }
        }
        Experts& X = Y.ex;
        X.nvfp4 = l < MXFP8_FIRST;
        X.w13 = (const uint8_t*) upload(ck.bytes(q + "mlp.experts.w13_weight"));
        X.s13 = (const uint8_t*) upload(ck.bytes(q + "mlp.experts.w13_weight_scale"));
        X.w2 = (const uint8_t*) upload(ck.bytes(q + "mlp.experts.w2_weight"));
        X.s2 = (const uint8_t*) upload(ck.bytes(q + "mlp.experts.w2_weight_scale"));
        if (X.nvfp4) {
            const vec a13 = ck.f32(q + "mlp.experts.w13_weight_amax"), d13 = ck.f32(q + "mlp.experts.w13_weight_outer_scale_denominator");
            const vec a2 = ck.f32(q + "mlp.experts.w2_weight_amax"), d2 = ck.f32(q + "mlp.experts.w2_weight_outer_scale_denominator");
            X.f13.resize(NE);
            X.f2.resize(NE);
            for (int e = 0; e < NE; ++e) { X.f13[e] = a13[e] / d13[0]; X.f2[e] = a2[e] / d2[0]; }
        } else if (ck.has(q + "mlp.experts.w13_weight_amax")) {
            std::fprintf(stderr, "layer %d: MXFP8 expected but the experts carry an amax\n", l);
            std::exit(1);
        }
        std::fprintf(stderr, "\rloaded layer %d/%d, %.1f GiB in VRAM", l + 1, NL, g_vram / 1073741824.0);
    }
    std::fprintf(stderr, "\n");
}

// Inkling2LoopSlidingAttention / Inkling2LoopFullAttention for one token at `pos`
vec attention(const Layer& Y, Slot& S, int pos, const vec& xq, const vec& xk, const vec& xv) {
    const float tau = log_scale(pos);
    vec q = mv(Y.wq, xq);
    for (int h = 0; h < NQ; ++h) {   // (Log)ScaledL2Clip
        float* v = &q[(size_t) h * HD];
        for (int d = 0; d < HD; ++d) v[d] *= Y.q_scale[(size_t) h * HD + d];
        l2_clip(v, HD, QK_CLIP);
        if (Y.full) for (int d = 0; d < HD; ++d) v[d] *= tau;
    }
    vec k = mv(Y.wk, xk), v = mv(Y.wv, xv);
    if (Y.full) {
        k = dyn_conv(k, xk, S.khist, Y.k_static, Y.k_wproj);
        v = dyn_conv(v, xv, S.vhist, Y.v_static, Y.v_wproj);
    } else {
        k = short_conv(k, S.khist, Y.k_conv);
        v = short_conv(v, S.vhist, Y.v_conv);
    }
    for (int g = 0; g < NKV; ++g) {
        l2_clip(&k[(size_t) g * HD], HD, QK_CLIP);   // keys: L2Clip, no scale
        vec vh(v.begin() + (size_t) g * HD, v.begin() + (size_t) (g + 1) * HD);
        vh = rms(vh, nullptr);                        // values: UnweightedRMSNorm per head
        std::copy(vh.begin(), vh.end(), v.begin() + (size_t) g * HD);
    }
    S.K.push_back(k);
    S.V.push_back(v);

    vec rs = mv(Y.wr, xq);   // relative position bias states [24][16]
    for (int h = 0; h < NQ; ++h) {
        float* r = &rs[(size_t) h * DREL];
        for (int d = 0; d < DREL; ++d) r[d] *= Y.rel_scale[(size_t) h * DREL + d];
        l2_clip(r, DREL, REL_CLIP);
        if (Y.full) for (int d = 0; d < DREL; ++d) r[d] *= tau;
    }
    const vec sink = mv(Y.sink, xq);
    const vec gate = mv(Y.gate_attn, xq);

    const int T = (int) S.K.size();   // == pos + 1
    const float scale = 1.0f / std::sqrt((float) HD);
    vec att((size_t) NQ * HD, 0.0f);
    for (int h = 0; h < NQ; ++h) {
        const int g = h / NREP;
        vec lg((size_t) T, -INFINITY);
        float mx = sink[h];
        for (int j = 0; j < T; ++j) {
            const int dist = (T - 1) - j;
            if (!Y.full && dist >= WINDOW) continue;
            float bias = 0.0f;
            if (dist < EXTENT) {
                for (int r = 0; r < DREL; ++r) bias += rs[(size_t) h * DREL + r] * Y.rel_proj[(size_t) r * EXTENT + dist];
            }
            lg[j] = dot(&q[(size_t) h * HD], &S.K[j][(size_t) g * HD], HD) * scale + bias;
            mx = std::max(mx, lg[j]);
        }
        float z = std::exp(sink[h] - mx);   // the sink: one more logit with a zero value
        for (int j = 0; j < T; ++j) { lg[j] = std::exp(lg[j] - mx); z += lg[j]; }
        float* o = &att[(size_t) h * HD];
        for (int j = 0; j < T; ++j)
            for (int d = 0; d < HD; ++d) o[d] += (lg[j] / z) * S.V[j][(size_t) g * HD + d];
    }
    for (size_t i = 0; i < att.size(); ++i) att[i] *= sigmoidf(gate[i]);   // GatedOutputProjection
    return mv(Y.wo, att);
}

vec moe(const Layer& Y, const vec& x) {   // Inkling2LoopSparseMoeBlock (router in fp32, sigmoid scores)
    const vec lg = mv(Y.router, x);
    vec s(NE), sel(NE);
    for (int e = 0; e < NE; ++e) { s[e] = sigmoidf(lg[e]); sel[e] = s[e] + Y.router_bias[e]; }
    std::vector<int> idx(NE);
    std::iota(idx.begin(), idx.end(), 0);
    std::partial_sort(idx.begin(), idx.begin() + TOPK, idx.end(), [&](int a, int b) { return sel[a] > sel[b]; });
    float sum = 0.0f;
    for (int k = 0; k < TOPK; ++k) sum += s[idx[k]];
    vec out(H, 0.0f);
    for (int k = 0; k < TOPK; ++k) {
        const int e = idx[k];
        const float w = s[e] / (sum + 1e-20f) * ROUTE_SCALE;
        const vec gu = expert_mv(Y.ex, e, true, x);
        vec act(FF);
        for (int j = 0; j < FF; ++j) {   // SoftcappedSwiGLU
            const float g = gu[2 * j], u = gu[2 * j + 1];
            act[j] = (std::tanh(g / GATE_CAP) * GATE_CAP) * sigmoidf(g) * (std::tanh(u / UP_CAP) * UP_CAP);
        }
        const vec y = expert_mv(Y.ex, e, false, act);
        for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) out[i] += y[i] * w;
    }
    return out;
}

// One decoder layer application; `msgs` = the 4 messages (q, k, v, mlp) on loop passes 1-2, else nullptr.
vec layer_forward(int l, Slot& S, int pos, const vec& h, const std::vector<vec>& B, const std::vector<vec>* msgs) {
    const Layer& Y = M->L[l];
    vec xq, xk, xv;
    if (Y.full) {
        vec qi = h, ki = h, vi = h;
        if (l > 0) {
            auto r = attnres(Y.attn_query.data(), 3, B, h);
            qi = r[0]; ki = r[1]; vi = r[2];
        }
        if (msgs) {
            qi = message_gate(Y.gates[0], qi, (*msgs)[0]);
            ki = message_gate(Y.gates[1], ki, (*msgs)[1]);
            vi = message_gate(Y.gates[2], vi, (*msgs)[2]);
        }
        xq = rms(qi, &Y.q_norm); xk = rms(ki, &Y.k_norm); xv = rms(vi, &Y.v_norm);
    } else {
        const vec a = l > 0 ? attnres(Y.attn_query.data(), 1, B, h)[0] : h;
        if (msgs) {
            xq = rms(message_gate(Y.gates[0], a, (*msgs)[0]), &Y.attn_norm);
            xk = rms(message_gate(Y.gates[1], a, (*msgs)[1]), &Y.attn_norm);
            xv = rms(message_gate(Y.gates[2], a, (*msgs)[2]), &Y.attn_norm);
        } else {
            xq = xk = xv = rms(a, &Y.attn_norm);
        }
    }
    vec att = attention(Y, S, pos, xq, xk, xv);
    att = short_conv(att, S.ahist, Y.attn_conv);
    std::vector<vec> B2 = B;
    vec r(H);
    if (l % BLOCK == 0) { r = att; B2.push_back(h); }
    else for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) r[i] = h[i] + att[i];
    vec m = attnres(Y.mlp_query.data(), 1, B2, r)[0];
    if (msgs) m = message_gate(Y.gates[3], m, (*msgs)[3]);
    vec y = moe(Y, rms(m, &Y.mlp_norm));
    y = short_conv(y, S.mhist, Y.mlp_conv);
    for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) y[i] += r[i];
    return y;
}

vec forward_token(int tok, int pos, std::vector<vec>* calls) {
    vec e(H);
    for (int i = 0; i < H; ++i) {
        uint32_t u = (uint32_t) M->embed[(size_t) tok * H + i] << 16;
        std::memcpy(&e[i], &u, 4);
    }
    vec h = rms(e, &M->embed_norm);
    std::vector<vec> B;
    auto run = [&](int l, int slot, const std::vector<vec>* msgs) {
        vec out = layer_forward(l, M->slots[slot], pos, h, B, msgs);
        if (l % BLOCK == 0) B.push_back(h);
        h = out;
        if (calls) calls->push_back(h);
    };
    for (int l = 0; l < LOOP_BEGIN; ++l) run(l, l, nullptr);
    const vec pre_h = h;
    const std::vector<vec> pre_B = B;
    for (int l = LOOP_BEGIN; l < LOOP_END; ++l) run(l, l, nullptr);
    for (int p = 1; p < PASSES; ++p) {
        const std::vector<vec> prevB(B.begin() + pre_B.size() + 1, B.end());   // +1: skip the first loop block
        const vec prev_h = h;
        h = pre_h;
        B = pre_B;
        for (int i = 0; i < NLOOP; ++i) {
            const std::vector<vec> msgs = attnres(&M->msg_queries[(size_t) i * 4 * H], 4, prevB, prev_h);
            run(LOOP_BEGIN + i, NL + (p - 1) * NLOOP + i, &msgs);
        }
    }
    for (int l = LOOP_END; l < NL; ++l) run(l, l, nullptr);
    h = attnres(M->final_query.data(), 1, B, h)[0];
    vec logits = mv(M->unembed, rms(h, &M->norm), VOCAB);
    for (float& v : logits) v = std::tanh(v / LOGIT_CAP) * LOGIT_CAP;
    return logits;
}

std::vector<int> parse_ids(const char* s) {
    std::vector<int> v;
    for (const char* p = s; *p;) {
        char* end = nullptr;
        v.push_back((int) std::strtol(p, &end, 10));
        if (end == p) break;
        p = *end == ',' ? end + 1 : end;
    }
    return v;
}

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    std::string dir, dump_layers, dump_logits;
    std::vector<int> ids;
    int n_gen = 16;
    for (int i = 1; i + 1 < argc; i += 2) {
        const std::string a = argv[i];
        if (a == "--model") dir = argv[i + 1];
        else if (a == "--tokens") ids = parse_ids(argv[i + 1]);
        else if (a == "--n") n_gen = std::atoi(argv[i + 1]);
        else if (a == "--pos-offset") g_pos_offset = std::atoi(argv[i + 1]);
        else if (a == "--tokens-file") {
            FILE* f = std::fopen(argv[i + 1], "r");
            if (!f) { std::fprintf(stderr, "cannot open %s\n", argv[i + 1]); return 2; }
            std::string text;
            for (int c; (c = std::fgetc(f)) != EOF;) text += (char) c;
            std::fclose(f);
            ids = parse_ids(text.c_str());
        }
        else if (a == "--dump-layers") dump_layers = argv[i + 1];
        else if (a == "--dump-logits") dump_logits = argv[i + 1];
        else { std::fprintf(stderr, "unknown option %s\n", a.c_str()); return 2; }
    }
    if (dir.empty() || ids.empty()) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "usage: bel-inkling2-ref --model DIR --tokens 1,2,3 [--n 16] [--dump-layers F] [--dump-logits F]\n");
        return 2;
    }
    Ckpt ck;
    ck.path = dir + "/model-00001-of-00001.safetensors";
    const std::string e = bel::safetensors::read_header(ck.path, ck.st);
    if (!e.empty()) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", e.c_str()); return 1; }
    g_ck = &ck;
    CK(cudaMalloc(&g_dx, (size_t) 2 * H * 4));
    CK(cudaMalloc(&g_dy, (size_t) 201024 * 4));
    load(ck);

    FILE* fl = dump_layers.empty() ? nullptr : std::fopen(dump_layers.c_str(), "wb");
    FILE* fg = dump_logits.empty() ? nullptr : std::fopen(dump_logits.c_str(), "wb");
    vec logits;
    for (size_t t = 0; t < ids.size(); ++t) {
        std::vector<vec> calls;
        logits = forward_token(ids[t], (int) t, &calls);
        if ((int) calls.size() != NCALLS) { std::fprintf(stderr, "%zu layer calls\n", calls.size()); return 1; }
        if (fl) for (const vec& c : calls) std::fwrite(c.data(), 4, H, fl);
        if (fg) std::fwrite(logits.data(), 4, logits.size(), fg);
        std::fprintf(stderr, "\rprompt %zu/%zu", t + 1, ids.size());
    }
    std::fprintf(stderr, "\n");
    if (fl) std::fclose(fl);
    if (fg) std::fclose(fg);
    std::printf("top:");
    {
        std::vector<int> o(logits.size());
        std::iota(o.begin(), o.end(), 0);
        std::partial_sort(o.begin(), o.begin() + 5, o.end(), [&](int a, int b) { return logits[a] > logits[b]; });
        for (int k = 0; k < 5; ++k) std::printf(" %d:%.4f", o[k], logits[o[k]]);
    }
    std::printf("\ngen:");
    int pos = (int) ids.size();
    for (int k = 0; k < n_gen; ++k) {
        const int next = (int) (std::max_element(logits.begin(), logits.end()) - logits.begin());
        std::printf(" %d", next);
        std::fflush(stdout);
        logits = forward_token(next, pos++, nullptr);
    }
    std::printf("\n");
    return 0;
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/ink2ref.cu =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/safetensors_lite.hpp =====
// src/image/vae/safetensors_lite.hpp - read the tensor table of a .safetensors file (the format: an 8-byte little-endian
// header length, a JSON object {name: {"dtype", "shape", "data_offsets": [begin, end]}, "__metadata__": {...}}, then the
// data).  Only what a model file of ours needs: string keys, the three fields above, numbers and arrays of numbers; the
// "__metadata__" object is skipped.  No third-party JSON code.  Header-only, host-only.
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <thread>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace bel::safetensors {

struct SafeTensor {
    std::string dtype;               // "BF16", "F16", "F32", ...
    std::vector<int64_t> shape;
    uint64_t begin = 0, end = 0;     // byte offsets from the start of the data section
};

struct SafeTensorFile {
    std::map<std::string, SafeTensor> tensors;
    uint64_t data_start = 0;         // file offset of the data section
};

namespace safetensors_detail {
struct Parser {
    const std::string& s;
    size_t i = 0;
    std::string err;
    explicit Parser(const std::string& t) : s(t) {}
    void ws() { while (i < s.size() && (s[i] == ' ' || s[i] == '\n' || s[i] == '\r' || s[i] == '\t')) ++i; }
    bool eat(char c) { ws(); if (i < s.size() && s[i] == c) { ++i; return true; } return false; }
    bool str(std::string& out) {
        ws();
        if (i >= s.size() || s[i] != '"') return fail("string expected");
        ++i;
        out.clear();
        while (i < s.size() && s[i] != '"') {
            if (s[i] == '\\') {
                if (i + 1 >= s.size()) return fail("bad escape");
                char e = s[i + 1];
                if (e == 'u') {                          // names are ASCII; keep \uXXXX as-is (never matched)
                    out += s.substr(i, 6);
                    i += 6;
                    continue;
                }
                out += (e == 'n') ? '\n' : (e == 't') ? '\t' : e;
                i += 2;
                continue;
            }
            out += s[i++];
        }
        if (i >= s.size()) return fail("unterminated string");
        ++i;
        return true;
    }
    bool num(int64_t& v) {
        ws();
        size_t j = i;
        if (j < s.size() && s[j] == '-') ++j;
        while (j < s.size() && s[j] >= '0' && s[j] <= '9') ++j;
        if (j == i) return fail("number expected");
        v = std::stoll(s.substr(i, j - i));
        i = j;
        return true;
    }
    bool skip_value() {               // any JSON value (for __metadata__ and unknown fields)
        ws();
        if (i >= s.size()) return fail("value expected");
        char c = s[i];
        if (c == '"') { std::string t; return str(t); }
        if (c == '{' || c == '[') {
            char close = c == '{' ? '}' : ']';
            ++i;
            if (eat(close)) return true;
            do {
                if (c == '{') { std::string k; if (!str(k) || !eat(':')) return fail("object"); }
                if (!skip_value()) return false;
            } while (eat(','));
            return eat(close) || fail("unterminated container");
        }
        while (i < s.size() && s[i] != ',' && s[i] != '}' && s[i] != ']') ++i;   // number, true, false, null
        return true;
    }
    bool int_array(std::vector<int64_t>& out) {
        out.clear();
        if (!eat('[')) return fail("[ expected");
        if (eat(']')) return true;
        do { int64_t v; if (!num(v)) return false; out.push_back(v); } while (eat(','));
        return eat(']') || fail("] expected");
    }
    bool fail(const char* m) { if (err.empty()) err = std::string(m) + " at byte " + std::to_string(i); return false; }
};
}  // namespace safetensors_detail

// Parse the header of `path` into `out`.  Returns an empty string on success, else the reason.
inline std::string read_header(const std::string& path, SafeTensorFile& out) {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "rb");
    if (!f) return "cannot open " + path;
    unsigned char len8[8];
    if (std::fread(len8, 1, 8, f) != 8) { std::fclose(f); return "short file " + path; }
    uint64_t n = 0;
    for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k) n = (n << 8) | len8[k];
    if (n == 0 || n > (64u << 20)) { std::fclose(f); return "implausible header length in " + path; }
    std::string text(n, '\0');
    if (std::fread(&text[0], 1, n, f) != n) { std::fclose(f); return "short header in " + path; }
    std::fclose(f);
    out = SafeTensorFile{};
    out.data_start = 8 + n;
    safetensors_detail::Parser p(text);
    if (!p.eat('{')) return "header is not a JSON object";
    if (p.eat('}')) return "";
    do {
        std::string name;
        if (!p.str(name) || !p.eat(':')) return "header: " + p.err;
        if (name == "__metadata__") { if (!p.skip_value()) return "header: " + p.err; continue; }
        SafeTensor t;
        if (!p.eat('{')) return "header: tensor entry is not an object";
        if (!p.eat('}')) {
            do {
                std::string key;
                if (!p.str(key) || !p.eat(':')) return "header: " + p.err;
                if (key == "dtype") { if (!p.str(t.dtype)) return "header: " + p.err; }
                else if (key == "shape") { if (!p.int_array(t.shape)) return "header: " + p.err; }
                else if (key == "data_offsets") {
                    std::vector<int64_t> o;
                    if (!p.int_array(o) || o.size() != 2 || o[0] < 0 || o[1] < o[0]) return "header: bad data_offsets";
                    t.begin = (uint64_t) o[0];
                    t.end = (uint64_t) o[1];
                } else if (!p.skip_value()) return "header: " + p.err;
            } while (p.eat(','));
            if (!p.eat('}')) return "header: } expected";
        }
        out.tensors[name] = t;
    } while (p.eat(','));
    if (!p.eat('}')) return "header: } expected at the end";
    return "";
}

// Read the whole data section (one sequential read; the decoder converts from memory).
inline std::string read_all(const std::string& path, const SafeTensorFile& file, std::vector<unsigned char>& bytes) {
    uint64_t end = 0;
    for (const auto& kv : file.tensors) end = kv.second.end > end ? kv.second.end : end;
    FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "rb");
    if (!f) return "cannot open " + path;
    bytes.resize(end);
#if defined(_WIN32)
    int rc = _fseeki64(f, (long long) file.data_start, SEEK_SET);
#else
    int rc = fseeko(f, (off_t) file.data_start, SEEK_SET);
#endif
    size_t got = rc == 0 ? std::fread(bytes.data(), 1, bytes.size(), f) : 0;
    std::fclose(f);
    return got == bytes.size() ? "" : "short read in " + path;
}

// The same into a buffer that is not zero-filled first, read in `threads` parts at once (each part its own file
// handle).  size = the data section's length.
inline std::string read_all_parallel(const std::string& path, const SafeTensorFile& file, std::unique_ptr<unsigned char[]>& buf,
                                        size_t& size, int threads) {
    uint64_t end = 0;
    for (const auto& kv : file.tensors) end = kv.second.end > end ? kv.second.end : end;
    size = (size_t) end;
    buf.reset(new unsigned char[size ? size : 1]);
    const int parts = threads < 1 ? 1 : (threads > 16 ? 16 : threads);
    const size_t step = (size + parts - 1) / parts;
    std::vector<std::string> errs((size_t) parts);
    std::vector<std::thread> pool;
    for (int p = 0; p < parts; ++p) {
        pool.emplace_back([&, p]() {
            const size_t b = (size_t) p * step, e = b + step < size ? b + step : size;
            if (b >= e) return;
            FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "rb");
            if (!f) { errs[(size_t) p] = "cannot open " + path; return; }
#if defined(_WIN32)
            int rc = _fseeki64(f, (long long) (file.data_start + b), SEEK_SET);
#else
            int rc = fseeko(f, (off_t) (file.data_start + b), SEEK_SET);
#endif
            size_t got = rc == 0 ? std::fread(buf.get() + b, 1, e - b, f) : 0;
            std::fclose(f);
            if (got != e - b) errs[(size_t) p] = "short read in " + path;
        });
    }
    for (auto& t : pool) t.join();
    for (const auto& e : errs)
        if (!e.empty()) return e;
    return "";
}

// Read the raw bytes of one tensor.
inline std::string read_tensor(const std::string& path, const SafeTensorFile& file, const SafeTensor& t,
                                  std::vector<unsigned char>& bytes) {
    FILE* f = std::fopen(path.c_str(), "rb");
    if (!f) return "cannot open " + path;
    bytes.resize(t.end - t.begin);
#if defined(_WIN32)
    int rc = _fseeki64(f, (long long) (file.data_start + t.begin), SEEK_SET);
#else
    int rc = fseeko(f, (off_t) (file.data_start + t.begin), SEEK_SET);
#endif
    size_t got = rc == 0 ? std::fread(bytes.data(), 1, bytes.size(), f) : 0;
    std::fclose(f);
    return got == bytes.size() ? "" : "short read in " + path;
}

}  // namespace bel::safetensors

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/inkling2/safetensors_lite.hpp =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/server/main.cpp =====
int llama_server(int argc, char ** argv);

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {
    return llama_server(argc, argv);
}

// ===== END SOURCE FILE: tools/server/main.cpp =====

// ===== BEGIN SOURCE FILE: tools/server/server-chat.cpp =====
#include "server-chat.h"
#include "server-common.h"

#include <sstream>

json server_chat_convert_responses_tools(const json & tools) {
    if (!tools.is_array()) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("'tools' must be an array of objects");
    }
    json chatcmpl_tools = json::array();
    for (json resp_tool : tools) {
        json chatcmpl_tool;

        const std::string type = json_value(resp_tool, "type", std::string());
        if (type != "function") {
            // Non-function Responses tools have no Chat Completions equivalent.
            SRV_WRN("unsupported Responses tool type '%s' skipped\n", type.c_str());
            continue;
        }
        resp_tool.erase("type");
        chatcmpl_tool["type"] = "function";

        if (!resp_tool.contains("strict")) {
            resp_tool["strict"] = true;
        }
        chatcmpl_tool["function"] = resp_tool;
        chatcmpl_tools.push_back(chatcmpl_tool);
    }
    return chatcmpl_tools;
}

json server_chat_convert_responses_to_chatcmpl(const json & response_body) {
    if (!response_body.contains("input")) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("'input' is required");
    }
    if (!json_value(response_body, "previous_response_id", std::string{}).empty()) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("llama.cpp does not support 'previous_response_id'.");
    }

    const json input_value = response_body.at("input");
    json chatcmpl_body = response_body;
    chatcmpl_body.erase("input");
    std::vector<json> chatcmpl_messages;

    if (response_body.contains("instructions")) {
        chatcmpl_messages.push_back({
            {"role",    "system"},
            {"content", json_value(response_body, "instructions", std::string())},
        });
        chatcmpl_body.erase("instructions");
    }

    if (input_value.is_string()) {
        // #responses_create-input-text_input
        chatcmpl_messages.push_back({
            {"role",    "user"},
            {"content", input_value},
        });
    } else if (input_value.is_array()) {
        // #responses_create-input-input_item_list

        static auto exists_and_is_array = [](const json & j, const char * key) -> bool {
            return j.contains(key) && j.at(key).is_array();
        };
        static auto exists_and_is_string = [](const json & j, const char * key) -> bool {
            return j.contains(key) && j.at(key).is_string();
        };

        for (json item : input_value) {
            bool merge_prev = !chatcmpl_messages.empty() && chatcmpl_messages.back().value("role", "") == "assistant";

            if (exists_and_is_string(item, "content")) {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-input_message-content-text_input
                // Only "Input message" contains item["content"]::string
                // After converting item["content"]::string to item["content"]::array,
                // we can treat "Input message" as sum of "Item-Input message" and "Item-Output message"
                item["content"] = json::array({
                    json {
                        {"text", item.at("content")},
                        {"type", "input_text"}
                    }
                });
            }

            if (exists_and_is_array(item, "content") &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "role") &&
                (item.at("role") == "user" ||
                    item.at("role") == "system" ||
                    item.at("role") == "developer")
            ) {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-item-input_message
                std::vector<json> chatcmpl_content;

                for (const json & input_item : item.at("content")) {
                    const std::string type = json_value(input_item, "type", std::string());

                    if (type == "input_text") {
                        if (!input_item.contains("text")) {
                            throw std::invalid_argument("'Input text' requires 'text'");
                        }
                        chatcmpl_content.push_back({
                            {"text", input_item.at("text")},
                            {"type", "text"},
                        });
                    } else if (type == "input_image") {
                        // While `detail` is marked as required,
                        // it has default value("auto") and can be omitted.

                        if (!input_item.contains("image_url")) {
                            throw std::invalid_argument("'image_url' is required");
                        }
                        chatcmpl_content.push_back({
                            {"image_url", json {
                                {"url", input_item.at("image_url")}
                            }},
                            {"type", "image_url"},
                        });
                    } else if (type == "input_file") {
                        throw std::invalid_argument("'input_file' is not supported by llamacpp at this moment");
                    } else {
                        throw std::invalid_argument("'type' must be one of 'input_text', 'input_image', or 'input_file'");
                    }
                }

                if (item.contains("type")) {
                    item.erase("type");
                }
                if (item.contains("status")) {
                    item.erase("status");
                }
                item["content"] = chatcmpl_content;

                chatcmpl_messages.push_back(item);
            } else if (exists_and_is_string(item, "role") &&
                item.at("role") == "assistant" &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "type") &&
                item.at("type") == "message"
            ) {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-item-output_message
                auto chatcmpl_content = json::array();

                // Handle both string content and array content
                if (item.contains("content") && item.at("content").is_string()) {
                    // String content - convert to text content part
                    chatcmpl_content.push_back({
                        {"text", item.at("content")},
                        {"type", "text"},
                    });
                } else if (exists_and_is_array(item, "content")) {
                    // Array content - process each item
                    for (const auto & output_text : item.at("content")) {
                        const std::string type = json_value(output_text, "type", std::string());
                        if (type == "output_text" || type == "input_text") {
                            // Accept both output_text and input_text (string content gets converted to input_text)
                            if (!exists_and_is_string(output_text, "text")) {
                                throw std::invalid_argument("'Output text' requires 'text'");
                            }
                            chatcmpl_content.push_back({
                                {"text", output_text.at("text")},
                                {"type", "text"},
                            });
                        } else if (type == "refusal") {
                            if (!exists_and_is_string(output_text, "refusal")) {
                                throw std::invalid_argument("'Refusal' requires 'refusal'");
                            }
                            chatcmpl_content.push_back({
                                {"refusal", output_text.at("refusal")},
                                {"type", "refusal"},
                            });
                        } else {
                            throw std::invalid_argument("'type' must be one of 'output_text' or 'refusal'");
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (merge_prev) {
                    auto & prev_msg = chatcmpl_messages.back();
                    if (!exists_and_is_array(prev_msg, "content")) {
                        prev_msg["content"] = json::array();
                    }
                    auto & prev_content = prev_msg["content"];
                    prev_content.insert(chatcmpl_content);
                } else {
                    item.erase("status");
                    item.erase("type");
                    item["content"] = chatcmpl_content;
                    chatcmpl_messages.push_back(item);
                }
            } else if (exists_and_is_string(item, "arguments") &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "call_id") &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "name") &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "type") &&
                item.at("type") == "function_call"
            ) {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-item-function_tool_call
                json tool_call = {
                    {"function", json {
                        {"arguments", item.at("arguments")},
                        {"name",      item.at("name")},
                    }},
                    {"id",   item.at("call_id")},
                    {"type", "function"},
                };

                if (merge_prev) {
                    auto & prev_msg = chatcmpl_messages.back();
                    if (!exists_and_is_array(prev_msg, "tool_calls")) {
                        prev_msg["tool_calls"] = json::array();
                    }
                    prev_msg["tool_calls"].push_back(tool_call);
                } else {
                    chatcmpl_messages.push_back(json {
                        {"role",       "assistant"},
                        {"tool_calls", json::array({tool_call})}
                    });
                }
            } else if (exists_and_is_string(item, "call_id") &&
                (exists_and_is_string(item, "output") || exists_and_is_array(item, "output")) &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "type") &&
                item.at("type") == "function_call_output"
            ) {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-item-function_tool_call_output
                if (item.at("output").is_string()) {
                    chatcmpl_messages.push_back(json {
                        {"content",      item.at("output")},
                        {"role",         "tool"},
                        {"tool_call_id", item.at("call_id")},
                    });
                } else {
                    json chatcmpl_outputs = item.at("output");
                    for (json & chatcmpl_output : chatcmpl_outputs) {
                        if (!chatcmpl_output.contains("type")) {
                            throw std::invalid_argument("Output of tool call missing 'type' field");
                        }
                        const auto type = chatcmpl_output.at("type");
                        if (type != "input_text" && type != "input_image") {
                            throw std::invalid_argument("Output of tool call should be 'Input text' or 'Input image'");
                        }
                        if (type == "input_text") {
                            chatcmpl_output["type"] = "text";
                        } else if (type == "input_image") {
                            if (!chatcmpl_output.contains("image_url")) {
                                throw std::invalid_argument("'image_url' is required");
                            }
                            chatcmpl_output["type"] = "image_url";
                            chatcmpl_output["image_url"] = json {
                                {"url", chatcmpl_output.at("image_url")}
                            };
                        }
                    }
                    chatcmpl_messages.push_back(json {
                        {"content",      chatcmpl_outputs},
                        {"role",         "tool"},
                        {"tool_call_id", item.at("call_id")},
                    });
                }
            } else if (exists_and_is_array(item, "summary") &&
                exists_and_is_string(item, "type") &&
                item.at("type") == "reasoning") {
                // #responses_create-input-input_item_list-item-reasoning

                if (!exists_and_is_array(item, "content")) {
                    throw std::invalid_argument("item['content'] is not an array");
                }
                if (item.at("content").empty()) {
                    throw std::invalid_argument("item['content'] is empty");
                }
                if (!exists_and_is_string(item.at("content")[0], "text")) {
                    throw std::invalid_argument("item['content']['text'] is not a string");
                }

                if (merge_prev) {
                    auto & prev_msg = chatcmpl_messages.back();
                    prev_msg["reasoning_content"] = item.at("content")[0].at("text");
                } else {
                    chatcmpl_messages.push_back(json {
                        {"role", "assistant"},
                        {"content", json::array()},
                        {"reasoning_content", item.at("content")[0].at("text")},
                    });
                }
            } else {
                throw std::invalid_argument("Cannot determine type of 'item'");
            }
        }
    } else {
        throw std::invalid_argument("'input' must be a string or array of objects");
    }

    chatcmpl_body["messages"] = chatcmpl_messages;

    if (response_body.contains("tools")) {
        const json chatcmpl_tools = server_chat_convert_responses_tools(response_body.at("tools"));
        chatcmpl_body.erase("tools");
        if (!chatcmpl_tools.empty()) {
            chatcmpl_body["tools"] = chatcmpl_tools;
        }
    }

    if (response_body.contains("max_output_tokens")) {
        chatcmpl_body.erase("max_output_tokens");
        chatcmpl_body["max_tokens"] = response_body["max_output_tokens"];
    }

    if (response_body.contains("reasoning")) {
        // Only "effort" is handled so far
        const json & reasoning = response_body.at("reasoning");
        if (reasoning.contains("effort")) {
            chatcmpl_body["reasoning_effort"] = reasoning.at("effort");
        }
        chatcmpl_body.erase("reasoning");
    }

    if (response_body.contains("text")) {
        const json & text = response_body.at("text");
        if (!text.is_object()) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("'text' must be an object");
        }

        chatcmpl_body.erase("text");
        if (text.contains("format")) {
            const json & format = text.at("format");
            if (!format.is_object()) {
                throw std::invalid_argument("'text.format' must be an object");
            }

            const std::string type = json_value(format, "type", std::string());
            if (type == "json_schema") {
                chatcmpl_body["response_format"] = {
                    {"type", "json_schema"},
                    {"json_schema", format},
                };
                chatcmpl_body["response_format"]["json_schema"].erase("type");
            } else if (type == "json_object" || type == "text") {
                chatcmpl_body["response_format"] = format;
            } else if (!type.empty()) {
                throw std::invalid_argument("'text.format.type' must be one of 'text', 'json_object', or 'json_schema'");
            }
        }
    }

    // llama.cpp params (e.g. seed, min_p) go under extra_params and are merged into the converted body
    if (response_body.contains("extra_params")) {
        chatcmpl_body.erase("extra_params");
        if (response_body.at("extra_params").is_object()) {
            for (const auto & [key, value] : response_body.at("extra_params").items()) {
                chatcmpl_body[key] = value;
            }
        }
    }

    return chatcmpl_body;
}

// Edits the cch section of an "x-anthropic-billing-header" system prompt.
// Does nothing to any other prompt.
//
// This is a claude message with a "cch=ef01a" attribute that breaks prefix caching.
// The cch stamp is a whitebox end-to-end integrity hint. It's not meaningful as a
// system prompt data, particularly to llama.cpp, but its presence means the prefix
// cache will not get past it: It changes on each request.
//
// Reference: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/21793
// Example header:
// ```
// x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_version=2.1.101.e51; cc_entrypoint=cli; cch=a5145;You are Claude Code, Anthropic's official CLI for Claude.
//                                                                            ^^^^^
// ```
static void normalize_anthropic_billing_header(std::string & system_text) {
    if (system_text.rfind("x-anthropic-billing-header:", 0) != 0) {
        return;
    }

    const size_t header_prefix_length = strlen("x-anthropic-billing-header:");
    const size_t cch_length = 5;
    const size_t index_cch = system_text.find("cch=", header_prefix_length);
    if (index_cch == std::string::npos) {
        return;
    }

    const size_t index_replace = index_cch + 4;
    if (index_replace + cch_length < system_text.length() && system_text[index_replace + cch_length] == ';') {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cch_length; ++i) {
            system_text[index_replace + i] = 'f';
        }
    } else {
        LOG_ERR("anthropic string not as expected: %s", system_text.c_str());
    }
}

json server_chat_convert_anthropic_to_oai(const json & body) {
    json oai_body;

    // Convert system prompt
    json oai_messages = json::array();
    auto system_param = json_value(body, "system", json());
    if (!system_param.is_null()) {
        std::string system_content;

        if (system_param.is_string()) {
            system_content = system_param.get<std::string>();
            normalize_anthropic_billing_header(system_content);
        } else if (system_param.is_array()) {
            for (const auto & block : system_param) {
                if (json_value(block, "type", std::string()) == "text") {
                    auto system_text = json_value(block, "text", std::string());
                    normalize_anthropic_billing_header(system_text);
                    system_content += system_text;
                }
            }
        }

        oai_messages.push_back({
            {"role", "system"},
            {"content", system_content}
        });
    }

    // Convert messages
    if (!body.contains("messages")) {
        throw std::runtime_error("'messages' is required");
    }
    const json & messages = body.at("messages");
    if (messages.is_array()) {
        for (const auto & msg : messages) {
            std::string role = json_value(msg, "role", std::string());

            if (!msg.contains("content")) {
                if (role == "assistant") {
                    continue;
                }
                oai_messages.push_back(msg);
                continue;
            }

            const json & content = msg.at("content");

            if (content.is_string()) {
                oai_messages.push_back(msg);
                continue;
            }

            if (!content.is_array()) {
                oai_messages.push_back(msg);
                continue;
            }

            json tool_calls = json::array();
            json converted_content = json::array();
            json tool_results = json::array();
            std::string reasoning_content;
            bool has_tool_calls = false;

            for (const auto & block : content) {
                std::string type = json_value(block, "type", std::string());

                if (type == "text") {
                    converted_content.push_back(block);
                } else if (type == "thinking") {
                    reasoning_content += json_value(block, "thinking", std::string());
                } else if (type == "image") {
                    json source = json_value(block, "source", json::object());
                    std::string source_type = json_value(source, "type", std::string());

                    if (source_type == "base64") {
                        std::string media_type = json_value(source, "media_type", std::string("image/jpeg"));
                        std::string data = json_value(source, "data", std::string());
                        std::ostringstream ss;
                        ss << "data:" << media_type << ";base64," << data;

                        converted_content.push_back({
                            {"type", "image_url"},
                            {"image_url", {
                                {"url", ss.str()}
                            }}
                        });
                    } else if (source_type == "url") {
                        std::string url = json_value(source, "url", std::string());
                        converted_content.push_back({
                            {"type", "image_url"},
                            {"image_url", {
                                {"url", url}
                            }}
                        });
                    }
                } else if (type == "tool_use") {
                    tool_calls.push_back({
                        {"id", json_value(block, "id", std::string())},
                        {"type", "function"},
                        {"function", {
                            {"name", json_value(block, "name", std::string())},
                            {"arguments", json_value(block, "input", json::object()).dump()}
                        }}
                    });
                    has_tool_calls = true;
                } else if (type == "tool_result") {
                    std::string tool_use_id = json_value(block, "tool_use_id", std::string());

                    auto result_content = json_value(block, "content", json());
                    if (result_content.is_string()) {
                        tool_results.push_back({
                            {"role", "tool"},
                            {"tool_call_id", tool_use_id},
                            {"content", result_content.get<std::string>()}
                        });
                    } else if (result_content.is_array()) {
                        // Single-pass: build both text and content_parts, decide format at the end
                        std::string result_text;
                        json content_parts = json::array();
                        bool keep_parts = false; // images or tool references

                        for (const auto & c : result_content) {
                            std::string c_type = json_value(c, "type", std::string());
                            if (c_type == "text") {
                                std::string text = json_value(c, "text", std::string());
                                result_text += text;
                                content_parts.push_back({
                                    {"type", "text"},
                                    {"text", text}
                                });
                            } else if (c_type == "image") {
                                keep_parts = true;
                                json source = json_value(c, "source", json::object());
                                std::string source_type = json_value(source, "type", std::string());
                                if (source_type == "base64") {
                                    std::string media_type = json_value(source, "media_type", std::string("image/jpeg"));
                                    std::string data = json_value(source, "data", std::string());
                                    std::string url = "data:" + media_type + ";base64," + data;
                                    content_parts.push_back({
                                        {"type", "image_url"},
                                        {"image_url", {{"url", url}}}
                                    });
                                } else if (source_type == "url") {
                                    content_parts.push_back({
                                        {"type", "image_url"},
                                        {"image_url", {{"url", json_value(source, "url", std::string())}}}
                                    });
                                }
                            } else if (c_type == "tool_reference") {
                                // tool search result naming a deferred tool
                                keep_parts = true;
                                content_parts.push_back({
                                    {"type", "tool_reference"},
                                    {"name", json_value(c, "tool_name", std::string())}
                                });
                            }
                        }

                        if (!keep_parts) {
                            // Text-only: collapse to a plain string for maximum compatibility
                            tool_results.push_back({
                                {"role", "tool"},
                                {"tool_call_id", tool_use_id},
                                {"content", result_text}
                            });
                        } else {
                            // images or tool references: keep array content parts
                            tool_results.push_back({
                                {"role", "tool"},
                                {"tool_call_id", tool_use_id},
                                {"content", content_parts}
                            });
                        }
                    } else {
                        tool_results.push_back({
                            {"role", "tool"},
                            {"tool_call_id", tool_use_id},
                            {"content", ""}
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!converted_content.empty() || has_tool_calls || !reasoning_content.empty()) {
                json new_msg = {{"role", role}};
                if (!converted_content.empty()) {
                    new_msg["content"] = converted_content;
                } else if (has_tool_calls || !reasoning_content.empty()) {
                    new_msg["content"] = "";
                }
                if (!tool_calls.empty()) {
                    new_msg["tool_calls"] = tool_calls;
                }
                if (!reasoning_content.empty()) {
                    new_msg["reasoning_content"] = reasoning_content;
                }
                oai_messages.push_back(new_msg);
            }

            for (const auto & tool_msg : tool_results) {
                oai_messages.push_back(tool_msg);
            }
        }
    }

    oai_body["messages"] = oai_messages;

    // Convert tools
    if (body.contains("tools")) {
        const json & tools = body.at("tools");
        if (tools.is_array()) {
            json oai_tools = json::array();
            for (const auto & tool : tools) {
                oai_tools.push_back({
                    {"type", "function"},
                    {"function", {
                        {"name", json_value(tool, "name", std::string())},
                        {"description", json_value(tool, "description", std::string())},
                        {"parameters", tool.contains("input_schema") ? tool.at("input_schema") : json::object()}
                    }}
                });
                if (tool.contains("defer_loading")) {
                    oai_tools.back()["defer_loading"] = tool.at("defer_loading");
                }
            }
            oai_body["tools"] = oai_tools;
        }
    }

    // Convert tool_choice
    if (body.contains("tool_choice")) {
        const json & tc = body.at("tool_choice");
        if (tc.is_object()) {
            std::string type = json_value(tc, "type", std::string());
            if (type == "auto") {
                oai_body["tool_choice"] = "auto";
            } else if (type == "any" || type == "tool") {
                oai_body["tool_choice"] = "required";
            }
        }
    }

    // Convert stop_sequences to stop
    if (body.contains("stop_sequences")) {
        oai_body["stop"] = body.at("stop_sequences");
    }

    // Handle max_tokens (required in Anthropic, but we're permissive)
    if (body.contains("max_tokens")) {
        oai_body["max_tokens"] = body.at("max_tokens");
    } else {
        oai_body["max_tokens"] = 4096;
    }

    // Pass through common params
    for (const auto & key : {"temperature", "top_p", "top_k", "stream", "chat_template_kwargs", "id_slot"}) {
        if (body.contains(key)) {
            oai_body[key] = body.at(key);
        }
    }

    // Handle Anthropic-specific thinking param
    if (body.contains("thinking")) {
        json thinking = json_value(body, "thinking", json::object());
        std::string thinking_type = json_value(thinking, "type", std::string());
        if (thinking_type == "enabled") {
            int budget_tokens = json_value(thinking, "budget_tokens", 10000);
            oai_body["thinking_budget_tokens"] = budget_tokens;
        }
    }

    // Handle Anthropic-specific metadata param
    if (body.contains("metadata")) {
        json metadata = json_value(body, "metadata", json::object());
        std::string user_id = json_value(metadata, "user_id", std::string());
        if (!user_id.empty()) {
            oai_body["__metadata_user_id"] = user_id;
        }
    }

    // llama.cpp params (e.g. seed, min_p) go under extra_params and are merged into the converted body
    if (body.contains("extra_params") && body.at("extra_params").is_object()) {
        for (const auto & [key, value] : body.at("extra_params").items()) {
            oai_body[key] = value;
        }
    }

    return oai_body;
}

json server_chat_msg_diff_to_json_oaicompat(const common_chat_msg_diff & diff) {
    json delta = json::object();
    if (!diff.reasoning_content_delta.empty()) {
        delta["reasoning_content"] = diff.reasoning_content_delta;
    }
    if (!diff.content_delta.empty()) {
        delta["content"] = diff.content_delta;
    }
    if (diff.tool_call_index != std::string::npos) {
        json tool_call;
        tool_call["index"] = diff.tool_call_index;
        if (!diff.tool_call_delta.id.empty()) {
            tool_call["id"]   = diff.tool_call_delta.id;
            tool_call["type"] = "function";
        }
        if (!diff.tool_call_delta.name.empty() || !diff.tool_call_delta.arguments.empty()) {
            json function = json::object();
            if (!diff.tool_call_delta.name.empty()) {
                function["name"] = diff.tool_call_delta.name;
            }
            if (!diff.tool_call_delta.arguments.empty()) {
                function["arguments"] = diff.tool_call_delta.arguments;
            }
            tool_call["function"] = function;
        }
        delta["tool_calls"] = json::array({ tool_call });
    }
    return delta;
}

json convert_transcriptions_to_chatcmpl(
        const json & inp_body,
        const common_chat_templates * tmpls,
        const std::map<std::string, uploaded_file> & in_files,
        std::vector<raw_buffer> & out_files) {
    // TODO @ngxson : this function may need to be improved in the future
    // handle input files
    out_files.clear();
    auto it = in_files.find("file");
    if (it != in_files.end()) {
        out_files.push_back(it->second.data);
    } else {
        throw std::invalid_argument("No input file found for transcription");
    }

    // handle input data
    std::string prompt          = json_value(inp_body, "prompt", std::string());
    std::string language        = json_value(inp_body, "language", std::string());
    std::string response_format = json_value(inp_body, "response_format", std::string("json"));
    if (response_format != "json") {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Only 'json' response_format is supported for transcription");
    }
    const common_chat_prompt_preset preset = common_chat_get_asr_prompt(tmpls, language);
    if (prompt.empty()) {
        prompt = preset.user;
    }
    if (!language.empty() && !preset.has_language) {
        prompt += string_format(" (language: %s)", language.c_str());
    }
    prompt += get_media_marker();

    json messages = json::array();
    if (!preset.system.empty()) {
        messages.push_back({{"role", "system"}, {"content", preset.system}});
    }
    messages.push_back({{"role", "user"}, {"content", prompt}});

    json chatcmpl_body = inp_body; // copy all fields
    chatcmpl_body["messages"] = messages;

    // because input from form-data, everything is string, we need to correct the types here
    std::string stream = json_value(inp_body, "stream", std::string("false"));
    chatcmpl_body["stream"] = stream == "true";

    if (inp_body.contains("max_tokens")) {
        std::string inp = inp_body["max_tokens"].get<std::string>();
        chatcmpl_body["max_tokens"] = std::stoul(inp);
    }

    if (inp_body.contains("temperature")) {
        std::string inp = inp_body["temperature"].get<std::string>();
        chatcmpl_body["temperature"] = std::stof(inp);
    }

    return chatcmpl_body;
}

json convert_speech_to_tts(const json & inp_body) {
    json tts_body = inp_body; // copy all fields

    // a custom voice is given as {"id": "..."}
    if (inp_body.contains("voice") && inp_body.at("voice").is_object()) {
        tts_body["voice"] = json_value(inp_body.at("voice"), "id", std::string());
    }

    // the default of the OpenAI API is mp3, we have no encoder for it
    const std::string response_format = json_value(inp_body, "response_format", std::string("wav"));
    if (response_format == "pcm") {
        tts_body["response_format"] = "pcm16"; // "pcm" is float32 in /tts
    } else if (response_format == "wav") {
        tts_body["response_format"] = "wav";
    } else {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Only 'wav' and 'pcm' response_format are supported for speech");
    }

    const std::string stream_format = json_value(inp_body, "stream_format", std::string());
    if (stream_format == "audio") {
        tts_body["stream"] = true;
    } else if (!stream_format.empty()) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("Only 'audio' stream_format is supported for speech");
    }

    if (json_value(inp_body, "speed", 1.0) != 1.0) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("'speed' is not supported for speech");
    }

    if (!json_value(inp_body, "instructions", std::string()).empty()) {
        SRV_WRN("%s", "'instructions' is not supported for speech and is ignored\n");
    }

    return tts_body;
}

llama_tokens server_chat_get_asr_prompt(const llama_model * model) {
    const char * src = llama_model_chat_template(model, "asr");
    if (src == nullptr) {
        return {};
    }
    const llama_vocab * vocab = llama_model_get_vocab(model);
    const llama_token bos = llama_vocab_bos(vocab);
    const common_chat_template tmpl(src, bos == LLAMA_TOKEN_NULL ? "" : common_token_to_piece(vocab, bos, true), "");
    jinja::context ctx(tmpl.source());
    jinja::global_from_json(ctx, json {
        {"bos_token",             tmpl.bos_token()},
        {"messages",              json::array()},
        {"add_generation_prompt", true},
    }, false);
    jinja::runtime runtime(ctx);
    const std::string prompt = jinja::runtime::gather_string_parts(runtime.execute(tmpl.prog))->as_string().str();
    return common_tokenize(vocab, prompt, false, true);
}
